He claims to be an odd-job man and former bouncer from the Eminent Domain in Jamrock, and he urges the police to open the gates for 'honest workers'. Your skills notice that something is off: his clothes don't fit, his slogans sound memorised, and he tenses up whenever the murder comes up.

Around him, a crowd of desperate scabs shout for work, money and their right to feed their families.

See also: [Scab](#scab), [Strike](#strike), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [Harbour](#harbour)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He is a mercenary in disguise, one of the dead man's comrades: Kortenaer, who later reappears in full armour at the tribunal.

</details>

### Shanky

*Also: Dennis, Shanks*

**A small, rat-faced Hardie Boy with two front teeth missing; he sucks up to Titus and heckles you at every turn.**

His real name is Dennis, and his mates also call him Shanks. He is a sneering, cunning man in his forties who sucks up to Titus Hardie and jeers at you whenever you question the gang.

The other Hardies give it back to him, mostly by teasing him about women and about still living with his mother.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie), [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

When violence breaks out at the tribunal, Shanky either dies or runs away, depending on how events go; if he runs, the surviving Hardie Boys swear to make him pay.

</details>

### Siileng

**A relentlessly upbeat street vendor from Siigay who runs an unlicensed stall by the traffic jam, selling clothes, sneakers, sunglasses and food parcels.**

Everything at Siileng's is 'cool' and '50% off'. He loves Revachol because no one needs a permit to sell anything, calls himself an independent entrepreneur, and says he sends half his profits to his grandma back home. His stock includes FALN sneakers, sunglasses, a sports visor and some old Samaran speakers he is ashamed of.

The racist lorry driver nearby accuses him of looting cargo.

See also: [Siigay](#siigay), [Samara](#samara), [Racist lorry driver](#racist-lorry-driver), [Ultraliberalism](#ultraliberalism)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Some of his goods are humanitarian aid: food kits marked as not for resale, from the Humanox aid lorry parked nearby, whose security seals have been broken.

</details>

### Sleeping Dockworker

*Also: Santiago S. John*

**A striking dockworker passed out at a table in the Whirling-in-Rags after a bottle or two of rum.**

His ID card names him Santiago S. John. With Interfacing you can pick his pocket for a little cash and his dockworker ID card; with enough shouting you can wake him. Awake, he explains the strike, praises Evrart, and warns that the dead man had dangerous friends who might come looking for trouble.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Union](#union), [Strike](#strike), [Interfacing](#interfacing)

### Smoker on the Balcony

*Also: Martin Martinaise*

**A handsome, evasive young man smoking on a balcony that overlooks the Whirling's backyard; he gives his name as 'Martin Martinaise'.**

He talks in a lowered voice, keeps glancing at the neighbouring windows and won't give his real name. He says someone more 'muscular' already asked him about the hanging, and he mentions spending Sunday night with a 'Sunday friend', who might be a witness.

He lives in apartment #28 of the building, and later turns up in the Whirling-in-Rags as well.

See also: [Sunday Friend](#sunday-friend), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

His Sunday friend is a Coalition official who did see something on the night of the hanging. The friend describes him as a young art student whose family emigrated from Kedra.

</details>

### Soona

*Also: Soona, the Programmer, Soona Luukanen-Kilde*

**A prickly, brilliant programmer who has set up her radiocomputer and strange equipment inside the abandoned church, researching something she won't easily explain.**

She is the former lead programmer of Fortress Accident, a failed radio game studio, and of RSA Radios, and calls herself a circuit bender. She has surrounded herself with basins and hydrotransducers, trying to measure a silence in the church and find where it begins.

The ravers who want the church for a nightclub need her to move, and she will cooperate only if you help her research first, starting with recovering a backup from her old studio in the Doomed Commercial Area.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer), [Project Dread Board](#project-dread-board), [Pale](#pale), [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Her research points to an entroponetic anomaly in the church, which you come to describe as a two-millimetre hole in reality. She can also help you get a radio signal through to Coalition Warship Archer.

</details>

### Steban

*Also: Steban, the Student Communist*

**A young, combative communist student who runs a secret night-time reading group in a disused corner of the Capeside Apartments.**

He and his fellow student Ulixes meet only at night, and Steban is paranoid enough to demand passphrases at the door; Cindy the Skull can point you to him. He has expelled most of the group over ideological heresies and loves to argue theory, but underneath he is sincere and gentler than he first seems.

A communist-leaning detective will find plenty to talk about with him.

See also: [Communism](#communism), [Kras Mazov](#kras-mazov), [Dialectics](#dialectics), [Revolution](#revolution)

### Sulisław Zawisza

*Also: Sulisław, Zawisza, Sulislaw*

**The Zsiemsk-born lead designer of 'Wirrâl Untethered', a radiocomputer game made by the studio Fortress Accident.**

His name is signed on a note left in a fridge, telling a colleague where to find the project's off-site copy. A project report blames him for last-minute additions, such as a secret mystical location at the very edge of the game's map.

Soona, who worked on the same project, calls him the project lead and grumbles about his feature creep.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer)

### Sunday Friend

*Also: Charles Villedrouin*

**A polished Coalition official from Sur-la-Clef whom you meet in apartment #28, home of the Smoker on the Balcony; he may have seen something on the night of the hanging.**

He introduces himself as Charles Villedrouin, an official of the Coalition Government working for the 'Institute of Price Stabilité'. He is eager to explain inflation targets, EPIS and the benefits of peace and prosperity, and stresses that he is only in Martinaise unofficially.

He speaks warmly but vaguely of his young 'friend', and you may run into him again strolling on the coast.

See also: [Smoker on the Balcony](#smoker-on-the-balcony), [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Moralism](#moralism)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He saw part of the night's events from the balcony: around half past midnight a group of men carried what looked like a body into the yard while the surrounding windows went dark. He never saw the hanging itself or heard any shots.

</details>

### Sylvie

*Also: Sylvie Malaìika*

**The Whirling-in-Rags' former waitress, who quit just before you woke up; you can reach her on the radio and hear exactly how badly you behaved.**

She left because of the rotting body in the yard, an awkward moment with Garte, and above all your drunken antics: waving your gun and badge around, smashing a stuffed skua, wrecking your room, clogging the toilet with police documents and singing the same sad song over and over.

She is nervous on the line, mostly worried about getting in trouble with the Union, and would rather you never called again.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Garte](#garte), [Kineema](#kineema), [Union](#union)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

She actually liked Garte and was flattered when he asked for her number; she asks you to tell him she is sorry.

</details>

### Tequila Sunset

*Also: Officer Sunset, Officer Tequila Sunset, Tequila*

**A grand name Harry gave himself during the drinking binge that wiped his memory; once you rediscover it, you can choose to go by it.**

You start the game not knowing it, but your skills drop hints, and people who met you during the binge, like Idiot Doom Spiral, still call you 'Tequila'. Once you know the name you can introduce yourself with it, and even use it as your callsign on the radio, where you become 'Officer Sunset'.

The name is a pun on the Tequila Sunrise cocktail: as several voices point out, this is a sunset, not a sunrise, because its owner is burning out.

*Real-world inspiration:* The Tequila Sunrise cocktail.

See also: [Personal Brand Strategist](#personal-brand-strategist), [Raphaël Ambrosius Costeau](#raphaël-ambrosius-costeau)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Idiot Doom Spiral can tell the whole tale of how Tequila Sunset rolled into Martinaise the Friday before and partied with him, but only after you have found out what happened to your motor carriage.

</details>

### The Gardener

**A polite young woman with a wheelbarrow at the corner by the Whirling-in-Rags; she gives directions and can lend you gloves and smelling salts.**

She says she is working on the gardens by the yard but keeps taking breaks because of the smell of the corpse. Ask her for directions around Martinaise, borrow her rubber gloves for the autopsy, or get ammonia salts for the stench. She mentions that the Wild Pines company woman is in town.

See also: [Union](#union), [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie), [Evrart Claire](#evrart-claire)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

She is not a gardener: she is Elizabeth 'Lizzie' Beaufort, a legal counsellor for the Dockworkers' Union (Evrart paid for her schooling) who has been keeping an eye on you, and she reappears at the Whirling to steer you towards Titus. She is badly hurt if the tribunal turns violent.

</details>

### The Hanged Man

*Also: hanged man, the corpse, Pretty Boy*

**The murder victim: a man found hanging from a tree in the yard behind the Whirling-in-Rags.**

Solving who he was and how he died is the main case of the game. He has been hanging there for days; the investigation should have started long before you woke up.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He was a mercenary commander who arrived with the company's security force. He was already dead when he was hanged: someone shot him first.

</details>

### The Pigs

*Also: Auntie LePlante, Marianne LePlante, Miss LePlante*

**A confused old woman who haunts the old fish market on the boardwalk at night, buried in scavenged police gear and yelling police jargon through a megaphone.**

She calls herself 'the Pigs' and is convinced she is a police officer. She wears a mountain of RCM paraphernalia with battery-powered sirens and beacon lights strapped to her back, barely registers the people around her, and keeps calling out for a 'Mr. Morrand'.

See also: [Evrart Claire](#evrart-claire), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie), [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

She has your missing service pistol: Evrart Claire arranges for you to meet her at the old fish market at night, where she points the three-barrelled gun at you; it turns out to be unloaded. Afterwards you can ask the Hardie Boys to look after her. Titus remembers her as 'Auntie LePlante' (Marianne LePlante), an old woman who did right by the dockworkers when they were kids; her own children moved away and never visit. 'Mr. Morrand' is Gareth Morrand, a former cop turned crime-show host on Channel 8 radio.

</details>

### Theo

**The oldest and quietest of the Hardie Boys: a silver-haired dockworker in his fifties who smokes and watches from the back of their room at the Whirling.**

Theo speaks slowly and rarely, and when he does it is blunt. He has a grim bet running that you will end up killing yourself, and says so in an almost kind voice. Like Eugene, he is of Areopagite descent.

If you are after the spare workshop key, he can fetch it from the tree outside the window and slide it across the table, while insisting he couldn't care less about your key.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie), [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Areopagite](#areopagite)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Theo can be among the Hardie Boys killed in the shootout at the tribunal; Kim remarks that he was simply too old for that kind of fight.

</details>

### Tiago

*Also: crab-man, Crab Man*

**A wiry man who lives in the rafters of the old church, carving wooden figures and urging you to give up drink for the love of 'the Mother'.**

The speedfreaks camped outside call him the crab-man because of the way he scuttles along the beams; he prefers to think of himself as a flame. He is Mesque and speaks in Mesque slang. He says he was once in a gang and a heavy drinker, but his memories of that life are fading, which he sees as a blessing.

He calls himself a saeraff who sings the silent glory of the Mother of Silence, and he keeps telling you that the bottle is wrecking your mind and spirit.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He tells you there is a perforation in the world up near the church ceiling, a way out into nothingness, and that the church was built around it. He circles it, hoping to become pure enough to drink from it one day. He also knows the password to the radiocomputer of the programmer working in the church.

</details>

### Tibbs

*Also: Tiberius*

**Titus Hardie's brother, a family man with a window-fitting business who keeps out of the Hardie Boys' affairs.**

Tibbs is short for Tiberius. Eugene says he has a wife, two kids and a third on the way, and has refused Titus's attempts to recruit him for years.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Klaasje](#klaasje), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Tibbs helped Fat Angus replace the broken window in Klaasje's room, part of the Hardie Boys' effort to cover up what really happened.

</details>

### Tiphaine Holly

*Also: Holly*

**The dockworkers' Union's forewoman before Evrart Claire. She stepped down after one term and vanished from public life.**

Joyce Messier knows her only as 'Holly' from company correspondence. On the last day of the local elections, her daughter supposedly phoned to say she would not run again or ever come back to work. Joyce finds this suspicious, and Bird's Nest Roy remembers her as rather more pleasant than Evrart, wondering where she went.

See also: [Evrart Claire](#evrart-claire), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Union](#union), [Wild Pines](#wild-pines)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The Deserter despised her as a bourgeois deal-maker. He hints that, around twenty years ago, she was shot and fell into a canal on her way home, as part of a deal with Edgar Claire, and that the call from her 'daughter' was faked. He never quite admits to pulling the trigger himself.

</details>

### Titus Hardie

*Also: Titus*

**The leader of the Hardie boys, a crew of union dockworkers who drink at the Whirling-in-Rags.**

Titus is a big, gruff union man (his vest says he captains a rowing club) who speaks for the Hardie boys and is protective of his crew. He insists they act on their own: Evrart runs the Union, Titus runs the Hardie boys.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Union](#union)

### Tommy Le Homme

*Also: Tommy, Jerry Lefitte*

**A lorry driver stuck for a week in the traffic jam outside the harbour gates; he passes the time writing rhymes and missing his family.**

Tommy hauls FALN sportswear (mostly tracksuits) and has to guard his cargo while the strike keeps the gates shut. He wants to become a musician, half-spoken verse over beats, and warms to you if you show some poetic talent. 'Tommy Le Homme' is a stage name; his real name is Jerry Lefitte.

His wife and child, with a second on the way, live in Deora-of-the-Seven-Seas on the island of Laurentide, at the far end of Le Caillou. He used to take drugs but says he has been clean for some time. He is friends with the 'lady driver' whose lorry stands abandoned in the jam.

See also: [Strike](#strike), [Harbour](#harbour)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The lady driver left him the keys to her lorry before she disappeared. He insists she is no drug trafficker and would rather not help you find her.

</details>

### Trant Heidelstam

*Also: Trant*

**A friendly, endlessly knowledgeable man you meet on the boardwalk with his young son, eager to explain the hidden history of West Martinaise.**

Trant is showing his son Mikael the ruined Feld Electrical building by the big mural. He explains that Feld built this side of Martinaise as a research centre during the turn-of-the-century cybernetics boom, and that the Coalition landed here in '08. He has something to say about almost everything, from tape computers and the Commune's March decree to Lo Manthang stick fighting, which he has practised for nearly twenty years.

He is pleasant company but lectures until you doze off. One of his asides can give you a Thought.

See also: [Mikael Heidelstam](#mikael-heidelstam), [Coalition](#coalition), [Commune](#commune), [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He turns up again at the very end of the case as a special consultant brought in by the RCM, offering theories about your amnesia and very interested in the public-relations value of what you have found.

</details>

### Ulixes

*Also: Uli, Comrade Ulixes*

**Steban's sidekick in the communist reading group at the Capeside Apartments, nicknamed Uli. He echoes and backs up whatever Steban says.**

He is the one member of the reading group whom Steban has not excommunicated, and the game simply labels him the Echo Maker: an intellectual hanger-on who repeats Steban's points, sometimes to Steban's irritation. Steban says Uli's father is from Gottwald and that Ulixes is especially interested in capitalist violence.

The two students argue theory, test how much 'plasm' they can muster between them, and play board games such as one about managing your own Zone of Ecological Catastrophe. Cindy the SKULL knows them both.

See also: [Communism](#communism), [Kras Mazov](#kras-mazov), [Yekokataa](#yekokataa)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The bylines in the student zine La Fumée look like pseudonyms; Logic suspects they are anagrams of Steban and Ulixes.

</details>

### Ulla Deponte

*Also: Deponte*

**A woman from a local legend and an old police case: in autumn '46 she was fishing by the water lock with Eva Delgado when Delgado vanished into the canal.**

Idiot Doom Spiral tells it as a ghost story: a sudden downpour, a scream, and the silhouette of the Headless FALN Rider on the horizon. Eva slipped into the canal and her body was never found. Kim knows it as the Deponte-Delgado case from the case files.

See also: [Union of Moribund Alcoholics](#union-of-moribund-alcoholics), [Fishing village](#fishing-village), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

According to Kim, Deponte became the prime suspect, since the police theory was a love triangle and a fight on the jetty. She insisted the Headless FALN Rider was to blame, then killed herself before she could be taken into custody.

</details>

### Washerwoman

*Also: Isobel, Isobel Sadie*

**An old, half-blind woman doing laundry in the Fishing Village; she knows everyone there and can rent you a room in her shack.**

Her name is Isobel, Sadie by marriage. Born in Samara, she has cataracts but a sharp ear and a sharper tongue, hums an old Samaran lullaby while she works, and is proud of the cinderblock village its people built for themselves. She offers you the spare room in her shack for free, as a gesture of goodwill to the RCM.

She distrusts the Union and the Claire brothers, and hers is one of the two signatures Evrart Claire needs for his plans in the village.

See also: [Evrart Claire](#evrart-claire), [Fishing village](#fishing-village), [Union](#union), [White Envelope](#white-envelope)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

She had rented the same room to Ruby and is protective of her. If you find enough evidence in the shack, you can confront her about it.

</details>

### Women's Rights

**A lone protester with a women's rights sign standing among the strikebreakers at the harbour gates, possibly at the wrong protest.**

The protester chants for equal wages, against domestic abuse and against being told to smile, and at one point wonders aloud whether this is the correct protest.

*Real-world inspiration:* The drunken mutterings you can hear later paraphrase Valerie Solanas's SCUM Manifesto (1967).

See also: [Scab](#scab), [Strike](#strike), [Picket line](#picket-line)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Later in the game the protester can be found lying drunk in the sand on the coast, mumbling anti-male slogans.

</details>

### Working Class Woman

**A tired woman browsing the soggy books at the book stand on the plaza; she insists her husband and daughters are not missing.**

She would rather read than talk to the police. Her daughters, she says, are safe at a party in Jamrock, and her husband went to the library yesterday morning to fetch her a book, promising to come straight home. Pushing her on it only irritates her.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

She is Billie Méjean, and her husband Victor is the dead man found on the Martinaise boardwalk. You can end up breaking the news to her at her flat in the apartment building.

</details>

### Émile Mollins

*Also: Mollins, Officer Mollins*

**A Precinct 41 patrol officer and partner of Chad Tillbrook, glimpsed only in Esprit de Corps visions of cops elsewhere in the city.**

In these visions Mollins whispers to Tillbrook about Tequila Sunset losing his mind in Martinaise, deals with a man who won't believe his dog is dead, and is ambushed in Porta Rosa, an alley off Boogie Street, while reaching for a cigarette for a stranger.

See also: [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Esprit de Corps](#esprit-de-corps), [Judit Minot](#judit-minot)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Late in the case, Judit Minot mentions she had Tillbrook and Mollins take care of the funeral arrangements for a body found in Martinaise.

</details>

### Õlari Taal

**The junior researcher who invented eternite at the Värtna Polytechnic Institute some thirty years ago. He thought it would last forever.**

That belief gave the material its name. According to Encyclopedia, the only lasting thing about eternite turned out to be its power to cause cancer.

See also: [eternite](#eternite), [Värtna Polytechnic Institute](#värtna-polytechnic-institute)

## Places

### 8/81

*Also: Motorway 8/81, Route 8/81*

**A huge elevated motorway running through Revachol; it separates Martinaise from Jamrock and casts its shadow over the slums beneath it.**

Motor carriages roar along it day and night, high above the rooftops. The streets underneath form a maze of alleys and ramshackle housing that is hard to get through on foot. The Eminent Domain lies in its shadow, and the Pox lies to the south of it.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Jamrock](#jamrock), [The Pox](#the-pox), [Revachol](#revachol)

### Abandoned Lorry

**An empty lorry cabin left in the traffic jam by the roundabout; its missing driver seems tied to a drug-smuggling operation.**

The locked cabin is full of cigarette smell, old posters of forgotten film starlets, a toolbox and a powerful radio transmitter. The other drivers talk about a 'lady driver' who has vanished, and Kim learns the lorry was used to move raw drug ingredients from Terminal B to Jamrock.

See also: [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Coalition Warship Archer](#coalition-warship-archer)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The lorry belongs to Ruby, the 'Instigator' the Hardie Boys mention. Sandpaper glued to the accelerator explains an oddly smooth boot print at the crime scene, placing her at the lynching. Its high-end radio transceiver can later help you reach Coalition Warship Archer.

</details>

### Advesperascit

**An old city in Vesper-Messina, the homeland of Dolores Dei, where she was crowned; the name means 'Evening comes'.**

It was one of the three Crown Cities of the Dolorian era on the Mundi isola, along with Deora and Rheasilvia. Its old town, sometimes called the Ceremonial Heart of Humanity, is all weathered marble, with thousands of columns and arcades around grand plazas, and its canals freeze in winter. Some say the city summoned Dolores Dei, or was built centuries in advance to prepare for her.

Vesper-Messina, the state it belonged to, has since split into the Republics of Vesper and Messina.

See also: [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Dolorian](#dolorian), [Innocence](#innocence)

### Banaital

**A jungle-covered island in the Semenese archipelago, near the pale, that served as a stronghold for Semenese nationalists in a proxy war.**

Encyclopedia explains the name when a veteran recalls a jungle reconnaissance mission there in the monsoon season of '41.

See also: [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The memory is prompted by the hanged man's tattoos, which refer to Banaital, '41: his old unit served there under his command, and his surviving squadmates defend the brutal things done there.

</details>

### Bay of Revachol

**The deep bay on which Revachol stands; it opens to the ocean and is crossed by company cargo ships.**

Martinaise looks out over the bay, which is more than 1200 metres deep at its deepest. Huge cargo ships bearing company logos such as Wild Pines, ZAMM and Moriyn cross it, a Coalition warship patrols its far reaches, and cold winds blow in from it through the city streets.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Harbour](#harbour), [Wild Pines](#wild-pines), [Coalition](#coalition)

### Boogie Street

**A long, lively and rough street that runs up through Revachol's poor districts to Jamrock, full of immigrants, music clubs, drug dens and gangs.**

Acele describes it as cool but bewildering, home to all kinds of people; poor kids from Faubourg walk its whole length up to Jamrock every spring for fun. It has a music club and synthesizer workshop where real musicians live, a charity school, and darker corners: the side alley Porta Rosa, where drugs change hands after midnight, and a drug den called 'The Door to the River'.

Its youth slang is catching on, and the Hardie Boys talk about 'Boogie Street justice'. Officers of Precinct 41 patrol it.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41)

### Boreal Plateau

**A frozen region in the far north of Katla, the coldest of the isolas; the smallest known cryptid, a bacterium, was discovered there.**

Lena explains that the bacterium Cryobacter katlensis was found at one of Katla's northernmost points by the geologist Catelyn Mijanou some 70 years ago. Encyclopedia adds that the communist revolutionary Ignus Nilsen once hid in a hut on the plateau for ten months, and Measurehead dreams of a superstate stretching all the way from Semenine to the Boreal Plateau.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Lena](#lena), [Isola](#isola)

### Caillou

*Also: Le Caillou, The Pebble*

**The large island on which Revachol stands; the name means 'pebble', and Martinaise lies on its north coast.**

Joyce Messier describes it as a smoothed, cracked pebble in a great blue sea; the cracks are the River Esperance, and Martinaise sits on the sixth branch of the river's delta. Encyclopedia notes that Le Caillou is a single fertile landmass, the fourth largest island in the world, unlike the neighbouring archipelagos of Ozonne and Face-à-La-Mer.

It was once the largest of the fertile, uninhabited islands of the northeast Insulindian archipelago; four centuries and two revolutions later it holds the city.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Isola](#isola)

### Capeside Apartments

*Also: Capeside, Capeside apartment buildings*

**The war-scarred tower block by the pier, next to the yard where the body hangs; several people involved in your case live there.**

Its address is Rue de Saint-Ghislaine 33A and 33B. Artillery fire shaved off its top floors, bullets are still lodged in its walls, and its blasted facade is wrapped in giant tarps and rusting scaffolding. The doors are locked, so getting inside takes some effort.

Its tenants include a pair of student communists, and a communist newsletter accuses the owner of trying to evict communist tenants for not paying rent. The library card found on the dead man on the boardwalk also leads to an apartment here.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Billie Méjean](#billie-méjean), [Communism](#communism)

### Central Jamrock Public Library

*Also: Jamrock Public Library, Jamrock Library*

**A public library in Jamrock that you can phone from the radio in Kim's motor carriage to trace the owner of a library card.**

A rain-soaked card from this library turns up in the pocket of a dead man on the Martinaise boardwalk. The card names the borrower and gives the library's address on Meroe Street in Jamrock.

Once you decide to follow the lead, the station operator can connect you to the library, where a polite male librarian looks the borrower up in the records for you. It only answers during opening hours.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Kineema](#kineema)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The card belongs to the dead man's wife, Billie Méjean. The librarian gives you her home address in Martinaise, in the Capeside Apartments, where the news has to be delivered.

</details>

### Coal City

**A destitute district of Revachol further along the coast, once a coal-mining boom town; by most accounts the worst place in the city.**

Shivers recalls its boom years, when coal from countless shafts powered Revachol. Once petroleum from the ocean floor and hydropower from the River Esperance replaced coal, everything collapsed, and only the weakest people remain among the defunct mines. Its black runoff stains the coast near Martinaise.

It was reduced to rubble in the war against the Commune, and Kim says gangs ferry amphetamines through it.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Jamrock](#jamrock)

### Coin-Operated Viewer

**Coin-operated tourist telescopes on the Martinaise plaza; one shows a half-sunken sea-fort in the bay, the other is jammed with old bubblegum.**

They are leftovers of a 1949 project to restore Martinaise to its pre-war glory, which Kim suspects the union pushed out. The working viewer costs a few centims and looks south at a ruined sea-fort on an islet, with a lone birch growing from its old anti-aircraft tower. The broken one faces the church across the water, its lens covered in graffiti.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Union](#union)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The sea-fort it shows becomes important very late in the case.

</details>

### Corpus Mundi

**A dormant shield volcano and, at 18.9 km, the highest summit in the world.**

Encyclopedia cheerfully offers this fact while admitting it has no idea what a field autopsy is, during your examination of the hanged man.

See also: [Mundi](#mundi), [Encyclopedia](#encyclopedia)

### Couron

*Also: the Couron*

**A lower-middle-class district of Revachol just east of Jamrock: not glamorous, but somewhere you can actually live.**

Shivers sees its seven-storey buildings cut into blocks by distributary after distributary of the river. The Coalition Government's courts, where the RCM sends the people it arrests, sit in Couron and La Delta.

René Arnoux fought communist partisans in Couron in the first months of the Revolution.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Revachol](#revachol), [Grand Couron](#grand-couron), [René Arnoux](#rené-arnoux)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Before joining the police, Harry was a high school gym teacher in Couron.

</details>

### Crime, Romance, and Biographies of Famous People

*Also: Crime, Romance & Biographies of Famous People, Biographies of Famous People*

**The little bookshop in the Doomed Commercial Area, run by the nervous Plaisance; it sells books, postcards and a few board games.**

Plaisance greets every visitor with the shop's full name, almost like a robot, while her daughter Annette stands outside to signal that the store is open. Plaisance insists that business is thriving and that the building has a 'magnetic' energy, not a curse.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

### Disco Elysium

**The title of the game, and also a name you can suggest for the dance club that Andre's crew wants to open in the old church.**

Noid connects the name to Elysium, the word for the whole world, while Acele wonders whether disco isn't a bit wacky and forgotten these days.

See also: [Elysium](#elysium), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

If you offer it when the club is finally named, Acele welcomes everyone to Disco Elysium as the lights sweep the dance floor.

</details>

### Dolorian Church of Humanity

*Also: the Church, Dolorian church, Small Pinewood Church*

**An old, abandoned church in Martinaise.**

The church was built for the Dolorian faith, named after the Innocence Dolores Dei, and has long since stopped being used for worship; some records call it the Small Pinewood Church. It is an old wooden stave church on the coast near the fishing village, one of les Sept Soeurs (the Seven Sisters) that once stood along the coast.

These days a group of young ravers want to turn it into a dance club.

See also: [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Dolorian](#dolorian), [Innocence](#innocence), [Fishing village](#fishing-village)

### Doomed Commercial Area

*Also: East Delta Commerce Centre*

**A commercial building in Martinaise where business after business has failed; locals believe it is cursed.**

Its official name is the East Delta Commerce Centre, on Rue de Saint-Ghislaine. Plaisance, who runs the bookstore on the plaza, is convinced the curse is real, and the way inside goes through her shop.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise)

### East Delta Pinball Arcade

*Also: East Delta Pinball*

**A failed pinball arcade that once filled the building now housing the Whirling-in-Rags. Its broken machines are still lying around.**

Theo, an older man at the Whirling, remembers it as a weird place that went bankrupt. The dead pinball cabinets, the freight elevator and a dusty repair workshop upstairs are all leftovers from it.

Its name is still listed on the doorbell panel in the Doomed Commercial Area, because both buildings were built as part of the same development.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

### Eminent Domain

**A poor, working-class district of Jamrock that lies in the shadow of the elevated Motorway 8/81.**

Encyclopedia notes it has next to no municipal infrastructure. The leader of the strikebreakers at the harbour gates says he comes from there.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Scab](#scab)

### Faubourg

*Also: Faubourger*

**A vast, poor district south of Jamrock: the largest ghetto in Revachol, and according to Kim possibly in the world.**

Kim calls it a massive banlieue; it is divided into eleven districts. Acele and most of her friends grew up there, and Kim plans to drive the hanged man's body there for processing.

Bird's Nest Roy moved to Martinaise to get as far from Faubourg as he could after the nuclear accident at the People's Pile.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Revachol](#revachol), [Emergency Relief Brigade](#emergency-relief-brigade)

### Feld building

*Also: Feld Mural*

**The gutted ruin of Feld Electrical's research centre on the coast near the boardwalk, with a faded mural that once read 'FELD ELECTRICAL R&D'.**

The abandoned building looms over the boardwalk: collapsed roof, broken windows, birds nesting in the walls. Its peeling mural still shows the company name and the ghost of a slogan, 'Tomorrow is just a whisper away'. Trant Heidelstam likes to stand nearby with his son and lecture about its history.

During the Revolution it was seized by revolutionaries. Your Shivers skill keeps pulling you back to it for reasons you cannot explain at first.

See also: [Feld Electrical](#feld-electrical), [Feld Playback Experiment](#feld-playback-experiment), [Shivers](#shivers)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The missing Ruby, the Instigator, is hiding inside the ruin; the Shivers clues and the local children point the way in.

</details>

### Fishing village

*Also: Illisible*

**A small, run-down fishing village on the Martinaise coast; it has no real name, though some call it Illisible.**

You reach it by crossing the canal's water lock, which is broken when the game begins. The washerwoman who lives there can give you a shack to sleep in, and the abandoned church stands nearby on the coast.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

### Grand Couron

**A quarter of Jamrock dominated by a failed real-estate development: monstrous new tower blocks that almost no one can afford to live in.**

Encyclopedia lists Grand-Couron among Jamrock's quarters. Shivers sees cold wind howling through its empty halls, homes built on delusions; one eight-storey ghost tower overlooks Central Jamrock. Plaisance at the bookstore dreams of moving there with her husband. The Grand Couron Arena hosts big boxing matches.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Couron](#couron), [Crime, Romance, and Biographies of Famous People](#crime-romance-and-biographies-of-famous-people)

### Harbour

*Also: Harbor, the harbour, the harbor, Terminal B*

**The Martinaise docks, controlled by the dockworkers' union and currently shut down by a strike.**

See also: [Union](#union), [Evrart Claire](#evrart-claire), [Strike](#strike), [Wild Pines](#wild-pines)

### Horseback Monument

*Also: Horseback Antenna, equestrian monument*

**The bronze statue of King Filippe III on horseback on the traffic island at the roundabout; it is restored to look as if it is being blown apart.**

The plaque calls him Filippe III, the Squanderer, son of Filippe II the Opulent and father of Filippe IV the Insane. Communards blew the statue apart during the Revolution; years later some designers ironically restored it, frozen in the moment of the explosion. It is unpopular in left-leaning Martinaise, and the king's chest is pocked with odd dents.

When you first see it, it is surrounded by scaffolding.

See also: [Revolution](#revolution), [Royalists](#royalists), [Idiot Doom Spiral](#idiot-doom-spiral), [Martinaise](#martinaise)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It can become the centrepiece of a get-rich scheme with Idiot Doom Spiral that remakes the monument, and you can climb it to use as a makeshift radio antenna to reach the Coalition Warship Archer.

</details>

### Hsin-Yao

*Also: Hsin-Yao Commune*

**A place in the old Safre Empire on the Samaran isola, now home to big factories; long ago it had its own short-lived communist commune.**

Joyce Messier says much of the cargo passing through the harbour comes from large Samaran factories in South Safre, Siigay and Hsin-Yao.

The Hsin-Yao Commune was a third outbreak of the world revolution, in the Safre Empire, put down in '06 and now almost forgotten. Its Triangong rifles were stamped 'For Safre, and for all mankind', and its revolutionaries sang 'Boy with a Scythe / Golden Sun'.

See also: [Revolution](#revolution), [Commune](#commune), [Communism](#communism)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

You learn about the Commune from the Deserter, whose Triangong rifle was military aid sent by the Hsin-Yao Commune to Revachol's communards.

</details>

### Ile du Fantôme

**Another name for the Semenine (Semenese) Islands, a distant southern archipelago; the name means 'Ghost Island'.**

The bookstore's wall map shows it far off in the southwest of the Insulindic ocean. Inland Empire imagines mist-shrouded islands full of eerie pygmies and wild shamans; Encyclopedia corrects it, calling it a beautiful place with a rich and unique culture.

### Industrial Harbour

*Also: Greater Revachol Industrial Harbour*

**Revachol's vast port and container district right next to Martinaise; most of it is the beat of Kim's Precinct 57.**

Shivers sees it as an artificial mountain range of shipping containers, holding immense wealth with poverty in its shadow. The dockworkers' Union wants a piece of it. Harry's 41st and Kim's 57th can't agree whether Martinaise belongs to Jamrock or to the Industrial Harbour, so neither precinct has really policed it.

See also: [Harbour](#harbour), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Precinct 57](#precinct-57), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Union](#union)

### International Zone

**A name for Revachol under foreign administration after the Revolution; Joyce says it was chosen because no nation wanted responsibility for the city.**

Kim says the Coalition Government formed the RCM to restore order in the International Zone after the Revolution, and that the militia now simply tries to keep that order. Joyce puts it differently: the RCM brought back peace where the Coalition had failed, as a citizens' initiative.

Jamrock is described as part of the International Zone.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Coalition](#coalition), [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Revolution](#revolution), [Jamrock](#jamrock)

### Jamrock

*Also: Jamrock Quarter*

**A rough district of Revachol, home of Harry's Precinct 41.**

See also: [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Revachol](#revachol)

### Karaoke Stand

*Also: karaoke stage, karaoke carousel*

**The karaoke stage in the Whirling-in-Rags cafeteria; Inland Empire badly wants you to sing on it.**

Garte keeps the 'karaoke carousel' switched off and calls it a prop, blaming a 'Great Karaoke Catastrophe of '44'. He will only plug it in for you once your bills are paid and you bring him a tape of a song to sing.

How the performance goes depends partly on the time of day: in the morning the hall is nearly empty, while in the evening the room is full of drinkers who may (or may not) listen.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Garte](#garte), [Inland Empire](#inland-empire)

### Koko Nur

**A vast, arid desert in South Samara, said to be home to the Giant of Koko Nur, the biggest cryptid of all.**

Lena says the Giant lives in the driest parts of the desert, casting a strange light over the wastes, and that local folklore treats its towering glow as a bad omen. Some think a gravity anomaly under the desert lets it grow so huge.

Morell has never been there and probably never will: the desert region has been caught in a small civil war for eight years.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Lena](#lena)

### La Cherte

**A great city in Sur-la-Clef, home to La Cherte University and namesake of the First La Cherte Accord, an international agreement.**

The angry author of the book 'Un Pays Infernal' teaches at La Cherte University. A Coalition official mentions that corporate espionage violates the First La Cherte Accord, and the Coalition warship's voice names La Cherte among the world's great cities.

See also: [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Coalition](#coalition)

### La Delta

**Revachol's wealthy financial district, whose golden skyscrapers you can see across the bay from Martinaise.**

It sits on the delta of the River Esperance, where countless bridges join city blocks to river islands. It is home to the stock exchange, office towers and Coalition Government courts; Evrart Claire's accountant works there too.

To the people of Martinaise it stands for money and power.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Coalition](#coalition)

### Land's End

**The northern tip of the coastal peninsula beyond the church, with reeds, an abandoned building and a disused radio relay tower.**

Lilienne says locals know it as a make-out spot and agrees to an evening stroll there. Morell set one of his cryptid traps behind a sand dune on the way to the old radio tower, and the washerwoman points real-estate types to the abandoned building.

See also: [Fishing village](#fishing-village), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

A rare brand of cigarette butts found there matches ones you can find later out on the sea fort island, a clue that someone has been moving between the two. You can also suggest its old radio relay tower as an antenna site for a radio project.

</details>

### Lausanne Central Aerodrome

*Also: Lausanne Aerodrome*

**Revachol's great airport: a cocoon hung by suspension cables above Midtown's skyscrapers across the bay, circled by aircraft.**

You see it in a Shivers vision of Midtown. It also comes up in one of your dreams, where someone must catch a flight from the Lausanne Aerodrome at 10:20 PM.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Aerostatic](#aerostatic)

### Le Jardin

**A rich area of Revachol East on the hills beyond the river, with a zoo, an aquarium, universities and courts.**

The bookstore map groups it with Stella Maris and Saint-Batiste as the wealthy suburbs of Revachol East. The Institute for Revacholian Culture has its architecturally significant headquarters there, and at the Hardie Boys' table you are told that guilt is for the courts in Le Jardin to decide.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol)

### Le Royaume

**Revachol's ancient catacombs, where for three centuries the city laid its blue-blooded dead to rest.**

Shivers senses doors leading down to it from the flooded storm drains, and reports the 'petits rats' braving underground passages from L'Ossuaire Municipal, the city's underground cemetery, to reach it. Cuno says he is going to move down there and live in a catacomb.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Royalists](#royalists), [Cuno](#cuno)

### Lelystad

**A farming municipality in Oranje, near the border of Gottwald; the hanged man came from there, which is where the nickname 'Lely' comes from.**

Klaasje explains that she called him Lely, short for Lelystad, and that it was his army name. Encyclopedia sums the place up as cows, silos and wheat.

*Real-world inspiration:* Lelystad, a real town in the Netherlands.

See also: [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Klaasje](#klaasje), [Oranje](#oranje)

### Magritte

*Also: River Magritte*

**A great river in Mesque. Its upper reaches ('upstream Magritte') are the steppes of Northern Mesque, home of the boiadeiro cow herders.**

Historically, the Innocence Dolores Dei's mass resettlement of upstream Magritte is remembered as one of her darker policies.

In the old ballad 'On the Western Plain', which the Paledriver describes, the river is where a boiadeiro's tragic love story comes to its grim end.

See also: [Mesque](#mesque), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei)

### Main Street

**A street in Jamrock, the district of Harry's Precinct 41.**

Shivers pictures Jamrock as a chessboard of wooden firetraps stretching from Main Street to Precinct 41 atop the motorway. Harry's old case files grumble that investigations keep stalling at the corner of Main Street and Perdition, where two troublemakers are always hanging out.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Rue Saint-Gérôme](#rue-saint-gérôme)

### Martinaise

*Also: North Jamrock*

**The poor harbour district of Revachol where the whole game takes place.**

Martinaise is a run-down neighbourhood of docks, shuttered shops, a fishing village and an abandoned church. The dockworkers' union is the real power here, and the district is in the middle of a strike.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Harbour](#harbour), [Union](#union), [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags)

### Mirova

**The capital of Graad, a city of 22 million people. Encyclopedia calls it the capital of the world since Revachol's fall.**

Its area code comes up when you use a payphone. Cuno claims his fancy trousers were made in Mirova by 'pants scientists'.

*Real-world inspiration:* Loosely Moscow, as Graad is loosely modelled on Russia and the Soviet Union.

See also: [Lu Jiatun](#lu-jiatun), [Revachol](#revachol)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

In a dream, a woman from Harry's past tells him she has left for Mirova and a new life there.

</details>

### Night City

*Also: City of Locusts, City of Rage, Locust City, Rage City, City of Locusts, City of Rage, Night City*

**Cuno's grand names for a 'city' he claims to be building; when you ask where he has been, he says he was in Night City.**

Ask Cuno where he has been and he says he was in Night City. Encyclopedia points out that no such city exists anywhere; it sounds like something from a pulp science fiction novel. Cuno gets furious if anyone calls the project lame, while Conceptualization senses real artistic tension in it.

See also: [Cuno](#cuno), [Cunoesse](#cunoesse), [Insulindian phasmid](#insulindian-phasmid)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The city is an insect town in a nearby shack: towers of mud and sand full of locusts that Cuno took from the cryptozoologist's phasmid traps. Finding it solves the 'missing locust' mystery and shows that it was Cuno, not the phasmid, who took the bait.

</details>

### Nightwatchman's Booth

**René Arnoux's spotless night-watchman's booth up on the harbour walkway, with his name on the door.**

Everything inside is meticulously organised. You can find René's very colourful old uniform folded on the table, a framed black-and-white photograph of a young couple at a street fair, and a box of out-of-date painkillers in a drawer.

See also: [René Arnoux](#rené-arnoux), [Harbour](#harbour), [Royalists](#royalists)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The young man in the photo is René himself, in a Royal Carabineer uniform. If you take the picture, you can ask him about it.

</details>

### Old Cinema

**A cinema building in Revachol that Shivers glimpses sinking into ruin beneath Villalobos, one of the quarters of Jamrock.**

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Shivers](#shivers)

### Old Military Hospital

**An abandoned hospital in the Pox, an overgrown sub-district of Jamrock right next to Precinct 41; most people avoid it.**

Shivers recalls that its grounds were once a park where patients recovered, until it became a quarantine centre during a measles outbreak in the Twenties that killed many children. Since then almost everyone has kept away from the hospital and its park, though heavy drug users still slip in, hoping to find something in the hastily abandoned supplies.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Shivers](#shivers), [Old South](#old-south)

### Old South

**One of the quarters that make up Jamrock, the district of Harry's precinct; an off-ramp of the elevated motorway 8/81 leads there.**

Encyclopedia lists Jamrock's quarters as the Pox, Villalobos, Central Jamrock, Grand-Couron, Old South and the Valley of the Dogs.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Old Military Hospital](#old-military-hospital)

### Ozonne

**A wealthy archipelago about two days' travel from Revachol, where the rich keep their châteaus; its people rarely set foot in Martinaise.**

Encyclopedia explains that its moneyed residents are used to posh restaurants and upscale boutiques. Joyce Messier recalls visiting Martinaise with her girlfriends from Ozonne in their shiny boats, and to people in Martinaise, from Cindy to Evrart Claire, the name stands for the rich who live far from the city's troubles. Its towns include Fond de l'Air and Virmandeux.

See also: [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Revachol](#revachol), [Insulinde](#insulinde)

### Paliseum

*Also: The Paliseum*

**A music club and synthesizer workshop on Boogie Street in Jamrock; to Acele and her friends it is the coolest place in the city.**

Real musicians live there, making the new synthesizer dance music and looping tape recordings of everyday sounds, like cracking ice or jangling keys. Acele got her tape recorder and microphone there and once spotted the musician Arno van Eyck. It is an open-minded place, and Noid and his friends make the pilgrimage north from Faubourg to visit it. Acele's group hopes to turn the old church into a club for the same kind of music.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Omicron](#omicron), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

### Pawnshop

*Also: pawn shop*

**A shop in a shack by the Martinaise canal and water lock, run by Bird's Nest Roy, where you can sell things for cash and buy odd gear.**

Kim and others point you there whenever you need money or equipment, such as a new tape player. Its owner, Roy, keeps a strange light show running inside and prefers natural sounds to music.

As Lena explains, pawning means handing over something valuable for money; you can buy it back later for a little more, if nobody has bought it first.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise), [reál](#reál)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Before the case began, you sold your own service pistol here to pay for drink.

</details>

### Perdition

*Also: Perdition and Main*

**A street in Revachol near Precinct 41; the corner of Perdition and Main is a familiar spot to the officers of Harry's station.**

Esprit de Corps shows Captain Pryce turning onto it as he rides home from the station, and Shivers follows the traffic from Eminent Domain to Perdition and on to Main Street.

See also: [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Ptolemy Pryce](#ptolemy-pryce)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Harry lives near there: Shivers glimpses his home as a vacant, nondescript building on the corner of Perdition and Main.

</details>

### Porta Rosa

**A side alley off Boogie Street where, after midnight, drugs and all kinds of 'funny things' are for sale, and sex too.**

Cindy the SKULL says she used to get anti-radiation pills there, sold by veterans of the People's Pile clean-up. Esprit de Corps glimpses an RCM patrol officer on duty in the alley.

See also: [People's Pile](#peoples-pile), [Jamrock](#jamrock)

### Precinct 41

*Also: 41st Precinct, 41st, Forty-first, Bloody Murder Station*

**Harry's home police station, in the district of Jamrock.**

See also: [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Jean Vicquemare](#jean-vicquemare), [Judit Minot](#judit-minot)

### Precinct 57

*Also: 57th Precinct, 57th*

**Kim Kitsuragi's police station.**

See also: [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

### Raehl

**A place with grand stairs that the Paledriver 'remembers' from the day of Franconegro's coronation, long before she was born.**

The Paledriver, an old lorry driver whose mind has been worn by years of pale crossings, says she remembers everything, even things she never knew. Among those memories: roses on the grand stairs of Raehl on Franconegro's coronation day, and holding her father's hand as he descended the stairs before a silent crowd. Then, she says, the memory was simply gone: pale damage.

See also: [Pale](#pale), [Innocence](#innocence)

### Revachol

*Also: Revacholian, Revacholians, Revachol West, Revacholiere*

**The city where the game takes place: a war-scarred former capital, now run by foreign powers.**

Revachol was once the seat of a powerful monarchy; Encyclopedia calls it the 'disgraced former capital of the world'. About fifty years ago a communist revolution took over the city and declared a Commune; an alliance of foreign nations, the Coalition, crushed it. Since then Revachol has been occupied and divided into zones of control, much of it poor and war-scarred, and still full of people who remember the Revolution.

The game takes place in one small district of it, Martinaise.

*Real-world inspiration:* Paris: the French-flavoured names, and the Commune, which echoes the Paris Commune of 1871.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Coalition](#coalition), [Revolution](#revolution), [Commune](#commune), [Insulinde](#insulinde)

### River Esperance

*Also: Esperance*

**The great river whose delta Revachol is built on; Martinaise lies on its sixth branch, the Martinaise distributary.**

The bookstore's wall map shows the river's delta shattering the island's north coast, with countless bridges joining city blocks to river islands and the district of La Delta at its heart. Precinct 57 stands on its south bank, and its hydropower helped put the old coal mines out of business.

It has had its disasters: the People's Pile, a failed particle decay generator, once leaked radioactive waste into it. The Coalition also runs a military prison named after it, the River Esperance Correctional Facility, which inmates call 'Reunion'.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Precinct 57](#precinct-57), [Coalition](#coalition)

### Roundabout North

**The name of the roundabout near the Whirling-in-Rags, with a horseback statue of an old king in the middle and lorries stuck in a traffic jam.**

The Gardener, who stands nearby with her wheelbarrow, tells you its name when you ask where you are. The lorry drivers stranded in the traffic jam and the statue of King Filippe III are here.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Martinaise](#martinaise)

### Rue de Saint-Ghislaine

*Also: Saint-Ghislaine*

**A major street of Martinaise: the Doomed Commercial Area is at No. 10 and the Capeside Apartments at Nos. 33A and 33B.**

Joyce Messier praises the street's old bastions and the restored streetlamps. Her boat is moored at the pier reserved for residents of number 33A, and a gnarled hawthorn tree grows on the street next to the canal.

When you use the radiocomputer in the Doomed Commercial Area, the repeater station greets you as 'Fortress Accident on Rue de Saint-Ghislaine', because a terminal there is still registered to that failed game studio.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier)

### Rue Saint-Gérôme

**A street in Revachol that Shivers glimpses in its visions of the city.**

In one vision it crumbles in the wind together with Main Street while the Old Cinema sinks beneath Villalobos; in another, a square bullet slides into a square chamber somewhere on it.

See also: [Shivers](#shivers), [Revachol](#revachol), [Main Street](#main-street)

### Saint-Batiste

**A former city absorbed into Revachol's sprawl, now part of the well-off eastern suburbs; it gives its name to a big pharmaceutical company.**

Its suburbs lie among the rolling hills of Revachol East alongside Le Jardin and Stella Maris, though it also has its slums: the boxer Contact Mike is said to have come from them. It hosted the Saint-Batiste Summer Dinghy Races, held only twice, in '31 and '33; Lena offers you her commemorative pin from the '31 races to pawn.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Saint-Batiste Pharmaceutics](#saint-batiste-pharmaceutics), [Lena](#lena)

### Samaran Butter

*Also: Samaran Butter sign, Samaran Butter Sign*

**A crumpled billboard advertising Samaran goat-milk butter that lies in the canal, jamming the water lock so you can't cross to the coast.**

The sign fell in recently: a speeding motor carriage used a shack roof as a ramp, tore through it and flew on into the fog. Visual Calculus can reconstruct the jump in detail and even guess the make of car, and Kim reminds you that you are not traffic cops.

Until the water lock is fixed, the canal blocks the way to the western coast and the fishing village.

See also: [Samara](#samara), [Sunken Motor Carriage](#sunken-motor-carriage), [Visual Calculus](#visual-calculus), [Fishing village](#fishing-village)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The tyre tracks are your own: you made the jump yourself during your binge, in your police Coupris 40, minutes before driving it into the sea.

</details>

### Scattering of Bullet Holes

**A crumbling wall on the western coast riddled with old automatic-rifle fire: the site of a mass execution during the Revolution.**

Visual Calculus can reconstruct the scene as ghostly figures: a dozen or more blindfolded people facing the wall, a firing squad of seven, and a commandant standing to one side. Who died here is unknown; Kim suggests it could have been royalists killed by communists, communists killed by the Coalition, or staff of the nearby Feld research centre.

Kim adds that fully automatic rifles were restricted after the Revolution because they proved too destructive.

See also: [Revolution](#revolution), [Visual Calculus](#visual-calculus), [Coalition](#coalition), [Commune](#commune)

### Sept Soeurs

*Also: les Sept Soeurs, Seven Sisters*

**'The Seven Sisters': seven old wooden stave churches that once stood on the coast. Most were destroyed; the church in Martinaise is a survivor.**

Kim explains that the others were burnt during the Revolution or taken apart for building materials. Accounts differ on whether one or two of the sisters still stand. The surviving church in Martinaise is a small, weather-beaten building put up by settlers from the Occident some 380 years ago.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Revolution](#revolution), [Occident](#occident)

### Stella Maris

**A wealthy hillside suburb of Revachol East, and one of the three footholds of Operation Death Blow in '08.**

The map in the bookstore places it among the rich eastern suburbs, along with Le Jardin and Saint-Batiste. René Arnoux says Martinaise, Stella Maris and the Delta were the three footholds used in Revachol during Operation Death Blow in '08, and Joyce Messier mentions the wealthy keeping châteaus there.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Martinaise](#martinaise)

### Sunken Motor Carriage

**A wrecked motor carriage lying half-sunk in the icy sea off the southern coast; the tyre tracks from the reckless canal jump lead here.**

Only the roof, rear wheels and engine show above the water. Kim estimates it went in over the weekend and suggests waiting for low tide to look inside, which you can do sitting on a nearby swing set.

See also: [Motor carriage](#motor-carriage), [Samaran Butter](#samaran-butter), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Kineema](#kineema)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It is your own police car, a Coupris 40 marked with the number 41 for your precinct. You drove it across the canal and into the sea during your binge, and it is a total write-off.

</details>

### Tabernacle Road

**A street in Jamrock where Lena lives; her address is 1113 Tabernacle Road.**

See also: [Lena](#lena), [Jamrock](#jamrock)

### Terminal H

**A cargo terminal on the south bank of the River Esperance. Kim's Precinct 57 stands near its gates.**

See also: [Precinct 57](#precinct-57), [Harbour](#harbour)

### The Pox

*Also: Pox*

**A wild, overgrown slum south of the 8/81 motorway, around an abandoned military hospital right next to Harry's Precinct 41.**

The area grew up around the Old Military Hospital, which later served as a goodwill hospital for shell-shocked veterans and as a quarantine centre during a deadly measles outbreak, before it was shut down. Today it is a maze of nameless streets, cinderblock shacks and thickets roamed by feral dogs and even wolves. Drug users hole up in the old hospital, and the police keep the deepest corners cordoned off.

See also: [8/81](#881), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Jamrock](#jamrock)

### Uamrao

**The world's largest canyon system, a dried-up riverbed in southeast Iilmaraa. Cryptozoologists say a giant bird was seen there.**

Lena explains that it lies east of the Erg desert and is vast, barren and mostly unexplored. It is where Dr. Plattenkalk, lost on a botanical expedition, says he followed the teratorn, a gigantic black bird.

See also: [Lena](#lena), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Cryptid](#cryptid)

### Video Revachol, 24h

*Also: Video Revachol, Video Revachol 24*

**A 24-hour film rental shop on the corner of Voyager and Main in Revachol; your fingers still remember its phone number.**

It rents eight- and ten-millimetre film for home use. From the punch-clock payphone in the dockworkers' office at the harbour, you can try dialling a number from muscle memory and reach Lemmy, the clerk on duty, who is not much help over the phone.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It is the rental place you and your ex-wife used to visit. You still have their copy of the bleak film 'Blue Ocean Hell', rented in November '46 and never returned.

</details>

### Villalobos

**A quarter of Jamrock built around a housing project, and also a peninsula in Mesque whose dialect many Mesque Revacholians speak.**

Jamrock is made up of Pox, Villalobos, Central Jamrock, Grand-Couron, Old South and the Valley of the Dogs. Encyclopedia recognises a Villalobos accent in Tiago's voice, and reads the fading gang tattoos of one of the Hardie Boys as a youth spent in the Villalobos projects, with time behind bars.

It also turns up in Harry's old case notes: Villalobos gang members once beat an RCM officer to death when he answered a call.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41), [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys)

### Voyager Road

*Also: Voyager and Main*

**A street in Revachol that keeps surfacing in your memories. The 24-hour Video Revachol rental shop stands on the corner of Voyager and Main.**

If you phone Video Revachol, the clerk tells you to come by the corner of Voyager and Main, and Shivers shows you the shop's neon sign in the rain. Inland Empire later offers a fleeting, happy image of you walking from Voyager Road each morning.

See also: [Jamrock](#jamrock)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Harry and Dora lived together at Voyager Road 21A, on the outskirts of Central Jamrock, when they were engaged; they used to rent videos at the shop at the end of the road. Her farewell note speaks of carrying a little piece of sadness down Voyager Road every morning.

</details>

### Vredefort

**The capital of Oranje: a conference city of parks, glass and duraluminium. Klaasje says she comes from there.**

Klaasje remembers it as a city of lilacs and lightning where it was always autumn and night. Vredefort has a military academy, a contemporary art centre with an absurd name (the Wompty-Dompty-Dom Centre), and the original men's workwear shop called Fritte, which is why the convenience store chain had to add a third 't'. It also turns up in radio weather reports.

See also: [Oranje](#oranje), [Klaasje](#klaasje), [Wompty-Dompty-Dom Centre](#wompty-dompty-dom-centre), [Frittte](#frittte)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The Hanged Man trained at the Ijsbrand Military Academy in Vredefort before serving in the Oranjese forces. Mercenaries' tattooed maps traditionally place Vredefort on the right shoulder.

</details>

### Värtna Polytechnic Institute

**A rather un-prestigious technical school where junior researcher Õlari Taal invented eternite about thirty years ago.**

See also: [Õlari Taal](#õlari-taal), [eternite](#eternite)

### Whirling-in-Rags

*Also: the Whirling, Whirling*

**The cheap hostel and cafeteria where you wake up. The body hangs in the yard behind it.**

A combined bar, cafeteria and hostel managed by Garte. You've been staying (and wrecking things) upstairs, and you need to pay for your room. Many important characters are inside or nearby.

See also: [Garte](#garte), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Martinaise](#martinaise)

### Wompty-Dompty-Dom Centre

*Also: Wompty-Dompty-Dom Centre for Contemporary Arts, Wompty-Dompty-Dom Centre of Arts*

**A contemporary art centre in Vredefort, Oranje, whose ridiculous name Trant Heidelstam drops with a perfectly straight face.**

Trant says he was a special consultant there for an exhibition ten years ago, working with head curator Paul Ockermann before the twins Keith and Guy Joost joined the team. Several of your skills cannot believe the name is real, but Trant insists you can visit it if you are ever in Vredefort and need an exhibition space.

See also: [Vredefort](#vredefort), [Oranje](#oranje)

### Yekokataa

*Also: Zone of Ecological Catastrophe*

**A Graadian farming megaproject turned desolate wasteland; the name shortens 'Zone of Ecological Catastrophe'. Egg Head insists it's 'the place to be'.**

Encyclopedia explains that Yekokataa is an abbreviation of a Graadian term meaning 'Zone of Ecological Catastrophe'. It was a giant agricultural project in the far south-east of the Graadian isola, built on experimental irrigation and a completely new kind of fertilizer. Today it is a desolate place of irrigation ditches and phosphorous mud that supports almost no plant or animal life.

Egg Head, one of the young ravers camped on the ice by the old church, keeps yelling that Yekokataa is 'the place to be' and a 'hard core' place. The racist lorry driver in the traffic jam claims it has lovely scenery and grows apples, which is plainly wrong. Steban the student communist even mentions a new board game about managing your own Zone of Ecological Catastrophe.

See also: [Communism](#communism), [Isola](#isola), [Ulixes](#ulixes)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The Deserter, an old hardline communist, thinks artists and revisionists belong in Yekokataa doing hard labour.

</details>

## World

### Aerostatic

*Also: aerostatics, aerostat, aerostats*

**An airship. Aerostatics are how people and goods fly across the world, even over the Pale.**

See also: [Pale](#pale)

### Areopagite

*Also: Areopagites*

**A people originally from Iilmaraa; the Hardie Boys Eugene and Theo are of Areopagite descent.**

Revachol once ruled a tobacco-exporting colony on Iilmaraa, Tioumoutiri, whose population was almost entirely Iilmaraan Areopagites. 'Kipt', once a common Areopagite first name, is now used as a slur against people of South Semenese or Areopagite descent. Measurehead has his own race-theory ideas about them.

See also: [Theo](#theo), [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

A witness's description of the men behind the hanging mentions two Areopagites and a Mesque, which could fit Eugene, Theo and Alain.

</details>

### Averro

*Also: Averros*

**A chain of high-end pharmacies found only in Vesper and Messina; a much nicer cousin of the humble Frittte.**

The signaller aboard the Coalition Warship Archer mentions that there are no Fritttes where the crew comes from; in Messina they have Averros instead. Encyclopedia fills in the rest: the company trades as Averro, SRL.

See also: [Frittte](#frittte)

### Barbara Muskova

**An expensive fashion label; Joyce Messier's silk shirt is a Barbara Muskova, a sign of the money she brings into poor Martinaise.**

Joyce mentions the label while admitting she has no right to complain about her comfortable life. Encyclopedia pairs it with Numb 12, the perfume she wears, as typical of her tastes.

See also: [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Tiphaine Holly](#tiphaine-holly)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The Deserter sneers that the Union's vanished former forewoman, Tiphaine Holly, carried a Barbara Muskova bag to work, proof to him that she was soft on the rich.

</details>

### Belle-Magrave

*Also: Belle-Magraves*

**A Revacholian rifle maker and its classic 4.46 calibre, breech-loading rifle, the standard weapon of the Revolution fifty years ago.**

Hand/Eye Coordination says the Belle-Magrave dominated the Insulindian battlefields of the Revolution, prized more for reliability than for accuracy. Because the bullet from the murder scene is an old 4.46 round, the rifle becomes an early lead in the case.

You can find a broken, inoperable one in Martinaise. René Arnoux, Titus Hardie and Kim all recognise it on sight, and Kim admires it as a beautiful relic of the Revolution.

See also: [Revolution](#revolution), [René Arnoux](#rené-arnoux), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The shot that killed the hanged man did come from an old 4.46 rifle, but the weapon turns out to be a different make of the same calibre; as Kim admits, nobody said it had to be a Belle-Magrave.

</details>

### Blaasma gun

*Also: blaasma, blaasma-powered weaponry*

**An absurd super-weapon your imagination invents: a gun firing periwinkle-blue 'blaasma' that detonates with the power of a dying star.**

When the station asks you to describe your missing service weapon, Inland Empire may urge you to describe the Blaasma Gun instead. You can also pitch the idea to an eccentric, fabulously rich stranger hiding in the harbour.

Kim, rubbing his eyes, points out that it is not even possible, and Logic agrees there is no such thing as blaasma. Gary the cryptofascist, on the other hand, lists blaasma-powered weaponry among the secret projects of his conspiracy theories.

See also: [Inland Empire](#inland-empire), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

### Burning Rhino

*Also: burning rhinos, burning rhinoceros*

**Gary's favourite cryptid: rhinos that blaze with fire in mating season, the males forming a flaming ring around the females.**

Gary places it somewhere in Safre Serai, though he has no intention of going there to look. Morell doubts it exists, and Kim notes drily that even the cryptozoologists draw the line somewhere.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology)

### Coalition Warship Archer

*Also: Archer, Warship Archer*

**A giant Coalition airship warship hovering over the Bay of Revachol, its guns ready to fire on the city at any sign of revolt.**

It is the flagship of the Coalition's INSURCOM forces in Revachol. Encyclopedia says it can fire fifty shells a minute that would reach the city in 58 seconds, and it has orders to fire on unidentified aerostatics that approach it. It keeps a discreet distance so that Revacholians don't feel militarily occupied.

The Archer also runs the Coalition's surveillance and communications in the city, so a few people suggest that contacting it by radio is the fastest way to reach the Coalition's Committee directly.

See also: [Aerostatic](#aerostatic), [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Moralism](#moralism), [Zone of Control](#zone-of-control)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

With help from Soona or Noid you can rig the old Horseback Antenna in Jamrock and actually raise the Archer, under a call sign of your choosing such as Firewalker. You end up chatting with a polite second signaller from Messina, and the Committee may even offer you an immediate extraction aboard an airship called the Laurel.

</details>

### Cor-de-Leite

*Also: Cor-de-Leite '19*

**Joyce Messier's white sloop, named after its type, a Cor-de-Leite '19 built by a Revachol East company of that name.**

Joyce explains that the boat has no name of its own: the company is Cor-de-Leite and the hull is 19 paces long. You can suggest names, but she politely sticks with the factory one. You first meet her beside it at the pier, and she likes it white.

See also: [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Harbour](#harbour)

### Coupris

*Also: Coupris MotorCorp, Coupris Motor Corporation, Coupris L'Ange*

**A motor carriage (car) brand: it makes both Kim's sleek Kineema and the common workhorse Coupris 40.**

Coupris MotorCorp built the Kineema as the follow-up to its hugely successful Coupris 40. According to Joyce Messier, the original company backed the king and went down with him; today Coupris is just a brand name owned by a Vespertine holding company.

The name also appears on a bottle of Coupris L'Ange engine starting fluid.

See also: [Kineema](#kineema), [Coupris 40](#coupris-40), [Motor carriage](#motor-carriage)

### Coupris 40

*Also: Coupris Forty*

**A sturdy, very common motor carriage model, driven by everyone from bank clerks to cops; one lies half-sunk in the sea off Martinaise.**

Encyclopedia calls it the workhorse of the motor park. Its successor is the Coupris Kineema that Kim drives, and the garages of Precinct 41 are full of them.

See also: [Coupris](#coupris), [Kineema](#kineema), [Precinct 41](#precinct-41)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The half-sunken Coupris 40 is your own police motor carriage. During your binge you drove it through a billboard by the canal and straight into the sea.

</details>

### Cryobacter katlensis

*Also: Cryobacter*

**The smallest known cryptid: a single-celled bacterium found frozen in the ice of Katla's Boreal Plateau about seventy years ago.**

Lena explains that the geologist Catelyn Mijanou discovered a colony that had survived frozen since before recorded history. Mijanou injected herself with it, apparently hoping to live longer; she stopped ageing but grew eccentric and irascible, drove away her friends and finally disappeared into the wilderness. Lena believes she may still be out there.

Morell respects Mijanou and regrets that her work on other cryptids is forgotten.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Lena](#lena), [Nnong Okk](#nnong-okk)

### Dolores Dei

**Her Innocence Dolores Dei: the founder of the humanist Dolorian era about four centuries ago, often called the greatest human being who ever lived.**

She began as councillor to a queen. After explorers came back from across the pale with news of a new continent, she was proclaimed an Innocence, the 'elected world spirit'. The age of humanism, internationalism and parliamentary rule that followed is called the Dolorian Era.

Icons often show her holding a tiny tin soldier, and Martinaise's abandoned church is a Dolorian Church of Humanity.

*Real-world inspiration:* Her name echoes Catholic devotion to the Virgin Mary as Our Lady of Sorrows: 'Dolores' is Spanish for 'sorrows' and 'Dei' is Latin for 'of God'.

See also: [Innocence](#innocence), [Dolorian](#dolorian), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

She was killed by one of her own bodyguards, who had come to suspect she was not entirely human. Some modern thinkers also consider her a war criminal for her campaigns against the Mesque state.

</details>

### Dolorian

*Also: Dolorianism, Dolorians*

**Relating to Dolores Dei: her era, the humanist tradition she began, and its churches and architecture.**

The Dolorian Era (or Dolorian century) followed her rise and is widely seen as the greatest age in history. Dolorianism is also a faith and a building style: Martinaise's abandoned church is a Dolorian Church of Humanity.

The Moralintern see themselves as Dolorians, carrying on her humanist project.

See also: [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Innocence](#innocence)

### Dread Moose

*Also: carnivorous moose*
