**A carnivorous moose from Arda that looks like any ordinary moose but eats only flesh; Morell calls it the most dangerous cryptid.**

According to Morell it even digs up fresh graves. His evidence includes slaughterhouse workers near Vaasa who kept seeing moose staring at the building with bloodshot eyes, and hikers who saw a moose nibbling a carcass. He believes it is a young species born of a mutation and worries what would happen if people saw moose as predators.

Encyclopedia notes that some deer scavenge anyway, and Logic suspects Morell is joking.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology)

### Drouin

**A popular cigarette brand: a local Revacholian blend made from tobacco grown on the southern islands.**

According to Encyclopedia, Drouin and Astra (a legendary cigarette from Graad) are what most Revacholians smoke today, while the old-fashioned Tioumoutiri brand is left to older men.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol)

### DynaWave

**Kim Kitsuragi's beloved radio set. He claims that turned up loud it's like sitting in the front row of the National Concert Hall.**

Whenever you come across a fancy radio, such as the Kristallsprach at the pawnshop, Kim stays loyal to his DynaWave, though Savoir Faire is sure he's a little jealous.

See also: [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

### East Motor Tract

**The road company that runs the motorway network and the drawbridge leading into Martinaise.**

At Wild Pines' request (the road company is a partner of one of its subsidiaries) the drawbridge has been raised, cutting the district's road link. Joyce Messier says it is meant to trap a smuggling operation she believes is funding the strike. Kim had to phone the company and beg to be let through when he drove in.

See also: [Wild Pines](#wild-pines), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi), [Martinaise](#martinaise)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Clues found in an abandoned lorry suggest that smugglers have infiltrated East Motor Tract's road network and are secretly using it to move goods.

</details>

### East-Insulindian Repeater Station

*Also: East-Insulindian Station, Yvonne*

**A radio relay service you reach through old radiocomputers; its operator, an elderly woman named Yvonne, unlocks stored files if you give the right password.**

When you press PLAY on a radiocomputer with a filament memory inside, a voice answers from the repeater station: Yvonne, 74, who sits in a cubicle surrounded by radios. Her job is to know where each registered terminal is and to guard its files with passwords.

The machines you call from in Martinaise are registered to a company called Fortress Accident (one in the old church, one in the Doomed Commercial Area). Yvonne asks you to confirm the name of the filament you inserted and then its password; get it right and she unlocks it so you can print the contents. Without the password, she says, you would need a warrant.

See also: [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

### Elysium

**The name for the whole world the game is set in, though people rarely use it.**

Elysium looks a lot like our 20th century (cars, radios, tower blocks, disco), but its geography, history and physics are its own. Its landmasses, the isolas, are separated by a strange grey void called the pale.

The word itself is seldom used; one character remarks that you don't hear that term often.

*Real-world inspiration:* In Greek mythology, Elysium (the Elysian Fields) is the paradise where heroes go after death.

See also: [Pale](#pale), [Isola](#isola)

### Entroponetics

*Also: entroponetic, entroponetical, Entroponetic Business Class*

**The modern science of the Pale, the grey void between the world's landmasses. 'Entroponetic' describes anything to do with it.**

Joyce Messier explains that people have studied the Pale for some 6,000 years (the ancient Perikarnassians called it the Western Plain) and that this latest form of the science came by way of Graad. In her view it has changed little: the only real progress is that aerostatics now cross the Pale faster, so travellers suffer less exposure.

Joyce flies 'Entroponetic Business Class', meaning she is cleared and trained for 22 days of Pale transit a year. Radio interference over long stretches of Pale is called entroponetic crosstalk.

See also: [Pale](#pale), [Aerostatic](#aerostatic), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier)

### eternite

**A cheap grey roofing material found all over Martinaise; it was meant to last forever but turned out to cause cancer.**

According to your Encyclopedia skill, eternite was invented some thirty years ago by a junior researcher named Õlari Taal at the unremarkable Värtna Polytechnic Institute. He named it for its supposed eternal durability; the only lasting thing about it proved to be its carcinogenic effect.

Eternite roofs and cinder blocks define the look of poor coastal Martinaise and the Fishing Village, and you will find loose panels of it in the Whirling's yard and on the beach.

*Real-world inspiration:* Eternit, a real brand of asbestos-cement roofing sheets; asbestos is a well-known cause of cancer.

See also: [Cuno](#cuno), [Martinaise](#martinaise), [Õlari Taal](#õlari-taal), [Värtna Polytechnic Institute](#värtna-polytechnic-institute)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The suspiciously neat pile of eternite at the back of the Whirling's yard hides a secret door into Cuno's shack.

</details>

### FALN

*Also: FALN AC*

**A brand of sporty 'performance' clothing (tracksuits, sneakers, caps) that also builds lorries. Kids in Revachol love it; adults find it juvenile.**

FALN gear turns up all over Martinaise. Cuno will sell you a pair of FALN Modular pants, a street vendor named Siileng offers the sleek FALN Ultra sneakers, and other pieces turn up elsewhere. Kim and Jean tease that the look makes you resemble a bicycle courier or a high-schooler.

The company also makes motor lorries, such as the FALN A-Z Tempo with its notoriously slippery throttle, and Cuno claims the pants are made in Mirova by 'pants scientists'.

See also: [Headless FALN Rider](#headless-faln-rider), [Cuno](#cuno), [Idiot Doom Spiral](#idiot-doom-spiral)

### Feld Electrical

*Also: Feld, Feld Electric*

**An electronics company that was once a giant of the cybernetics boom; it built the boardwalk side of Martinaise and now mostly sells ink cartridges.**

Trant Heidelstam tells you Feld began as a mid-sized electronics firm in Königstein about two centuries ago, moved aggressively to Revachol and became a world player in personal electronics before the Revolution. It built the boardwalk and that part of Martinaise to attract its star engineers, hoping to make it a global centre of cybernetics.

The Revolution ruined those plans: the revolutionary government seized Feld's assets and prototypes. Feld survives as a smaller company; radiocomputers still use Feld mainframes. Joyce counts it among the 'indotribes', Revachol's old giant companies.

See also: [Feld building](#feld-building), [Feld Playback Experiment](#feld-playback-experiment), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer), [Revolution](#revolution)

### Feld Playback Experiment

*Also: Feld Playback Experience*

**A legendary Feld Electrical prototype 'tape computer' that was lost when revolutionaries burned the company's research centre in '07.**

Trant Heidelstam describes it as an elegant, fragile machine: you wrote directly onto its tape with a special chemical solution, the machine read your handwriting, performed calculations and projected the result onto a white screen. It was Feld's secret weapon against its rival Tricentennial.

All three versions of the prototype were destroyed in the fires of '07. Trant insists the name is Experiment, not Experience.

See also: [Feld Electrical](#feld-electrical), [Feld building](#feld-building), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer)

### Filament memory

*Also: filament memories, filament memory units*

**A radiocomputer's data store: a glowing cube of fine filaments that slides into the machine's core and holds programs and records.**

Their contents are protected by passwords kept by a repeater station, which you contact through the radiocomputer. Older models were made of wires cast in glass.

You will need to find and handle filament memories to help Soona, the programmer working in the old church.

See also: [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

### Fortress Accident

*Also: Fortress Accident SCA, Fortress Accident on Saint-Brune*

**A failed radio game studio whose abandoned office sits in the Doomed Commercial Area; it burned through a fortune making 'Wirrâl Untethered'.**

Its official name was Fortress Accident SCA, and its catalogue blurb promised 'revolutionary interactive call-in radio games'. The 18-person team was mostly writers and concept artists, and its production schedule records a steady slide into bankruptcy. Soona, the programmer camped in the old church, was its lead programmer.

Radiocomputer terminals are still registered in the company's name: one in its old office on Rue de Saint-Ghislaine and one in the church on Saint-Brune. That is why the East-Insulindian Repeater Station greets you as 'Fortress Accident' when you use them.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The Mega Rich Light-Bending Guy in the container yard says the founders once pitched him a world-changing role-playing game, and that the name should have been a red flag. The studio's files also record a strange numeric anomaly on the radio network while their game was being compiled.

</details>

### Frittte

*Also: Fritttes*

**A chain of convenience stores (yes, with three t's). Martinaise has one.**

See also: [reál](#reál)

### Giant of Koko Nur

**The biggest cryptid of all: a colossal creature said to roam the Koko Nur desert in South Samara, casting a strange glow over the wastes.**

Lena calls it the largest animal the planet can support and suggests a gravity anomaly under the desert may let it grow so huge. Locals see its towering light as a bad omen, while others dismiss it as a mirage. Morell has never been there, since the region has been at war for years. People also use its name as a joke for anyone enormous.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Lena](#lena), [Insulindian phasmid](#insulindian-phasmid)

### Gnome of Geroma

**Reputedly the most dangerous cryptid ever: a small, ugly creature whose venom dissolved its victims so completely that their bodies were never found.**

Lena describes it as small and ungainly, with webbed fingers and a protruding forehead, and notes that it still took the Gnome an entire day to die. Science lost its only specimen when a researcher put the corpse in formaldehyde to neutralise the venom: the venom leaked out and dissolved the creature itself.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Lena](#lena)

### Gottwald

*Also: Gottwalder, Gottwalders, Gottwaldian, Gottwaldians*

**A wealthy foreign nation known for fine engineering, stationery and critical theory; its people are Gottwaldians.**

Gottwaldian things turn up everywhere: Bird's Nest Roy calls his Kristallsprach Model 9 radio Gottwaldian engineering at its finest, Lena credits the Gottwaldian explorer Uwe Plattenkalk with sighting the eight-eyed teratorn, and Encyclopedia knows a prize-winning Gottwaldian children's book. Students of the left argue about Gottwaldian critical theory and philosophers, and Drama hears a guttural Gottwaldian accent in Egg Head's yells.

Measurehead uses 'Maun', a derogatory word for first-world people of Gottwaldian descent.

See also: [Egg Head](#egg-head), [Gottwald School](#gottwald-school), [Oranje](#oranje)

### Graad

*Also: Graadian, Graadians*

**A huge, powerful nation on its own isola, with the world's largest capital, Mirova; the Revolution began there in '02.**

Graad lies on the Graadian isola, far to the northeast of Insulinde. Its capital Mirova has 22 million people and is now considered the capital of the world. The telephone was invented there, and Graad gave the world Astra cigarettes, the International Language and gloomy 'Graadian realism' novels.

The world revolution started in Graad in '02, with Kras Mazov heading its Eleven Day Government until he was overthrown in '08; today Graad is part of the Coalition that crushed Revachol's Commune. It is also fighting a long proxy war in South Safre. People from Graad are nicknamed Kojkos.

*Real-world inspiration:* Russia and the Soviet Union.

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Communism](#communism), [Isola](#isola), [Kras Mazov](#kras-mazov), [Revolution](#revolution), [Safre](#safre), [Yugo-Graad](#yugo-graad)

### Hydrodynamique E40

**A fast, silent and expensive sail; Joyce Messier says she would sooner buy one than hand you ten thousand réal.**

It comes up if you try to wheedle money out of Joyce on the pier: a new Hydrodynamique E40 sail and expensive hi-fi are her examples of things she'd rather spend her money on.

See also: [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [reál](#reál)

### Ice Bear Fridge

**A giant refrigerator shaped like a snarling ice bear, standing in a cellar in the Doomed Commercial Area; big enough to hold a body.**

It was once used to sell ice cream (crumpled 'Revachol Ice City' wrappers still lie inside), an unfortunate marketing choice since it terrified children. It is still plugged in and costing someone money.

It is the answer to a practical problem: the hanged man needs to be kept cold until he can be taken away. Kim agrees it is eccentric but perfect, and the two of you can carry the body down to it.

See also: [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

A note on its door points to an ice cream maker in the same cellar, where a frozen filament memory labelled 'OFF-SITE COPY' is hidden.

</details>

### Iilmaraa

*Also: Iilmaraan, Iilmaraans, Ile Marat*

**One of the isolas (continent-sized landmasses) of Elysium; once colonised and plundered by foreign powers, it is now an industrial powerhouse.**

Its ancestral name was Ile Marat, as on the Suzerainty board game, where it supplies archaeological treasure; in real life many Iilmaraan treasures still sit in Revachol, Gottwald and Sur-la-Clef. Revachol once ruled the tobacco colony of Tioumoutiri there, whose people were mostly Iilmaraan Areopagites, and the Mesque colonised it too.

Iilmaraan things crop up in odd places: Measurehead blames Iilmaraa for alcohol, the Iilmaraan diaspora carried funk music to Graad, and the dicemaker's phrase die comes from Iilmaraa, where it was used for fortune-telling.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Gottwald](#gottwald), [Graad](#graad), [Measurehead](#measurehead)

### Indotribe

*Also: indotribes, Indotribes*

**One of Revachol's fourteen original great companies, granted royal monopolies by the king centuries ago; Wild Pines is one of them.**

Most indotribes failed or were swallowed up, but several survive. Joyce Messier mentions Villiers & La Salle (artillery and ballistics), now owned by Gottwalders. She says ten of the fourteen, including Feld, Coupris and Tricentennial, got the revolutionary era wrong, and the survivors are all ultraliberals now.

Kim says Revachol's crime syndicates, the besmerties, like to see themselves as heirs of the indotribes.

See also: [Wild Pines](#wild-pines), [Royalists](#royalists), [Ultraliberalism](#ultraliberalism)

### Innocence

*Also: Innocences, innocentic, innocentic rule, innocentic system*

**A rare, sacred world leader elected as an embodiment of the 'world spirit'; only six have held the office in all of history.**

An Innocence is considered infallible: their decisions are treated as inevitabilities, history happening faster. They are elected by the Founding Party and addressed as 'Her Innocence' or 'His Innocence'.

The best known are Franconegro, crowned about 500 years ago, and Dolores Dei, about four centuries ago. The faces of past Innocences appear on reál banknotes.

*Real-world inspiration:* A mix of pope, emperor and messiah.

See also: [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Moralintern](#moralintern)

### Insulinde

*Also: Insulindian, Insulindic*

**The isola (landmass) where Revachol is located.**

*Real-world inspiration:* 'Insulinde' is a real, old poetic name for the islands of the Dutch East Indies (today's Indonesia).

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Revachol](#revachol), [Insulindian phasmid](#insulindian-phasmid)

### Insulindian phasmid

*Also: phasmid*

**A legendary cryptid: a giant stick insect said to live on Insulinde, which cryptozoologists hunt for.**

Most people consider it a myth, but Lena says she saw one as a child, and her husband Morell, a cryptozoologist, is searching for it.

See also: [Lena](#lena), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Insulinde](#insulinde)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It is real. You may meet it near the end of the game.

</details>

### Insulindic ocean

**The world's largest body of water; the island Revachol stands on lies in the middle of it, and Joyce calls it the world's connective tissue.**

The map wall in the Martinaise bookstore shows it as a pale blue emptiness dotted with archipelagos. The largest dot, in the northeast, is Le Caillou, where you are; far to the southwest lie the Semenese Islands. Other specks include Ozonne, Laurentide, Face-a-la-Mer and the North Arcade Islands.

The adjective 'Insulindic' turns up elsewhere too: Insulindic regattas, the Insulindic thrush, the East-Insulindic radio region.

See also: [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Isola](#isola), [Revachol](#revachol), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier)

### interisolary

**Between isolas: the in-world word for 'international' or 'intercontinental', as in interisolary travel or the interisolary reál.**

Because the world's landmasses are separated by the Pale, travel and trade between them are 'interisolary'. The Interisolary Age began with the discovery of Insulinde about three centuries ago. Examples: interisolary flights, the interisolary reál (IIR, the global reserve currency), the International Collaboration Police and the TipTop Tournée racing series.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Pale](#pale), [reál](#reál), [Aerostatic](#aerostatic)

### Isola

*Also: isolas, isolae*

**A continent-sized landmass, surrounded and separated by the Pale.**

Elysium's land is divided into isolas: great islands or continents cut off from each other by the Pale. Revachol is on the isola of Insulinde.

See also: [Pale](#pale), [Insulinde](#insulinde)

### Katla

*Also: Katlan*

**The coldest isola in the world: a far-off landmass of tundra and taiga, home to Vaasa and to peoples like the Suru.**

Joyce names Katla among the world's isolas, the coldest of them. It lies as far from Revachol as possible. In the far north, beyond the latitude called 'winter's orbit', days last 25 hours; your Encyclopedia describes it as a land of eternite, drunks and failed rock stars. The Boreal Plateau is there, and the indigenous Suru and Uuhu peoples live on it.

A fictionalised Katla is the setting of the Man from Hjelmdall pulp adventure books.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Vaasa](#vaasa), [Graad](#graad)

### Kedra

*Also: Kedran, Kedreatic*

**A large nation on the Mundi isola, north of the Occident; an emerging market and a contentious candidate for EPIS membership.**

'The Greatest Innocence' places it between the Irmalan mountain range and the Pisantic sea, and recalls Stepan the Despicable, regent of Kedra, who conquered the known world in the Kedreatic Conquest.

The Coalition official in the apartment building says his young friend's family emigrated to Revachol from Kedra, and that life has not been easy for them.

See also: [Isola](#isola)

### Kiejl

**A firearms maker; its mass-produced A9 'Armistice' muzzleloader is one of the most common guns in the world, and Kim carries one.**

Encyclopedia describes the A9/90 Armistice as plain, frugal and everywhere. Kiejl also makes the Model 40 revolver.

Kim's sidearm is an Armistice; Interfacing suggests it may once have been your own weapon of choice too.

See also: [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi), [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia)

### Kind Green Ape

*Also: green ape, Kind Green Ape of South Safre*

**A shy cryptid from South Safre said to slip into wartime bunkers at night and heal wounded soldiers with its saliva.**

Lena calls it half war story, half undiscovered species of the genus homo: a cousin that evolved alongside humans. Some argue it should count as human because it has proved so humane. It only approaches people to save lives, and no scientist, not even Morell, has tracked it down.

The ape is pictured on a pen you carry, and Morell says it is one of Lena's favourites.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Lena](#lena)

### Kineema

*Also: Coupris Kineema*

**The Coupris Kineema: Kim Kitsuragi's fast motor carriage (car), with a radio for calling the station and a toolbox he lets you use.**

Kineema is a model made by Coupris MotorCorp, a sportier successor to the Coupris 40, a workhorse long favoured by the police. Kim is quietly proud of his and protective of its delicate folding headlights.

See also: [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi), [Motor carriage](#motor-carriage)

### Kristallsprach

*Also: Kristallsprach Model 9*

**A Gottwaldian brand of fine radio transceivers; Bird's Nest Roy's pawnshop has an expensive Kristallsprach Model 9 for sale.**

Roy praises the Model 9 as a specialist's set, ideal for picking up foreign signals, with a signal purifier to cut crosstalk. Kim calls it a connoisseur's model but says he is happy with his own DynaWave.

It is one of the transceivers you can bring to people who need a good radio for their project.

See also: [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer)

### Königstein

*Also: Königsteiner*

**A wealthy, industrial foreign land known for its electronics industry and for fashionable, much-mocked psychological theories.**

The electronics firm Feld started out there two centuries ago, and the Whirling's old lift was made by a long-gone Königsteiner company. Kim lists it among the industrial places that make automated games.

Trant Heidelstam speaks about fringe theories developed there in the Thirties, such as partial psychotraumatic amnesia and group personality theory. Acele, out on the ice, dismisses its psychology as 'Königstein wank' that rich people like.

See also: [Amnesia](#amnesia)

### Lickra

**A shiny synthetic fabric, usually written with a trademark sign, used for flashy tracksuits. Encyclopedia is not impressed by it.**

Encyclopedia says Lickra was developed in the mid-'40s and is notable mostly for the swishing noise it makes when the wearer walks past. It adds that trials have linked it to several rare and nasty cancers.

The homeless former creative director who hangs around the fishing village wears a filthy two-piece Lickra tracksuit and still considers it premium material.

*Real-world inspiration:* Lycra (spandex), the stretchy synthetic sportswear fabric.

See also: [Sansarique](#sansarique), [Fishing village](#fishing-village)

### Linnea G22

*Also: Linnea*

**An unpopular model of motor carriage with peculiar proportions. Visual Calculus suspects one might have made a wild jump over the canal.**

When you reconstruct the crash at the canal, where a vehicle vaulted off a roof and wrecked a SAMARAN BUTTER billboard, Visual Calculus names two candidates: the Coupris 40 and the Linnea G22, a sturdy but light carriage more likely than most to survive such a jump.

See also: [Motor carriage](#motor-carriage)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The tyre tracks match your own: the car was actually your Coupris 40, minutes before it ended up in the sea.

</details>

### Lintel

**The company that licenses and runs the radio 'front' (the shared network of radiowaves) in the East-Insulindian region, carrying radiocomputer data.**

Radiocomputer operators send their data 'up to Lintel', and private data stored on the network can't be accessed without filing a warrant with them. A team of game makers who once worked in Martinaise spent weeks on the phone with Lintel after a mysterious error on its network destroyed their work, but the company never explained it or paid for the loss.

See also: [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer), [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Lintel could only give the coordinates of the error, which turned out to match a real physical place. Following that lead points to the old church on the coast, and later the programmer Soona tries to repeat Lintel's calculation with better equipment.

</details>

### Lo Manthang

**A distant group of isles, known for its rural tribes and a martial art fought with slender wooden sticks.**

Trant Heidelstam has practised Lo Manthang stick fighting for almost 20 years. According to Joyce, the Wild Pines fleet charted Lo Manthang some 250 years ago.

Encyclopedia notes that self-immolation has become a grim trend there, especially among young religious protesters and university students who oppose the Seraise government and creeping Occidental influence.

*Real-world inspiration:* The name comes from Lo Manthang, the old walled capital of the Mustang region in Nepal.

See also: [Wild Pines](#wild-pines)

### Lomonossov's Land

**One of the deep routes through the pale driven by the Paledriver, a pale-scarred long-haul lorry driver you meet in the traffic jam.**

She lists it with other routes, such as Udachnaya Zemlya and the Western Plain. She warns that somewhere in the pale superdeep, if you stray too far off course, there is a point where every step takes you further from home, whichever way you go.

See also: [Pale](#pale)

### Looskap

**A huge financial conglomerate. Encyclopedia says it engineers financial disasters in poorer countries.**

Its holdings include the Loosduin County Savings Bank, the Bank of Consecration, Eeirberg and the popular Pappa Lollo line of dairy products. Kim thinks going after Looskap would be unwise.

See also: [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Klaasje admits that one of her corporate-espionage jobs targeted a Looskap-owned bank, and that Looskap is now among the people after her.

</details>

### Major Majestic

*Also: Major Majestic cockatoo*

**A rare, endangered cockatoo with pink wings and a flowing crest, described as the most flamboyant and boastful bird in the jungle.**

You can read about it in a guide to keeping cockatoos, alongside the funeral cockatoo and the common bang-bang cockatoo. Harry can decide that the Major Majestic is the cockatoo for him.

*Real-world inspiration:* Major Mitchell's cockatoo, a real pink cockatoo with a showy crest.

### Mauer-Koshlai Mercury Group

*Also: Mauer-Koshlai Mercury, Mauer-Koshlai*

**A Gottwaldian-Iilmaraan company that extracts mercury. A very rich art buyer may offer you its shares instead of cash.**

The buyer says the company's profits keep growing thanks to the discovery of 'rödfärg' mercury. What he hands over is a stock certificate, though only a photocopy of one.

See also: [Ultraliberalism](#ultraliberalism)

### May bell

*Also: May bells, Insulindian Lily, Lucille's tears, May bell, Insulindian Lily, Lucille's tears*

**The Insulindian Lily: a white, bell-shaped spring flower. During the Revolution girls pinned it on soldiers going off to fight.**

Kim explains that the flower's old name is the Insulindian Lily and that it was also called May bells or Lucille's tears during the Revolution. Girls gave the flowers to young men leaving for battle; René remembers they were worn on the cap for luck. White was the colour of the revolutionaries, but the custom began in the royal army, so it meant something to both sides.

For Kim it is above all a symbol of the Civil War: of young people sent to die. It normally blooms in spring, so finding one in the cold before the season is strange.

See also: [Dried Wildflower](#dried-wildflower), [Revolution](#revolution), [René Arnoux](#rené-arnoux)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Whole fields of May bells grow on the islet with the old Sea Fort, where the Deserter lives; he left the dried ones on the Whirling roof.

</details>

### Megatherion

**The 'vision beast' of a drug-addled taxidermist: the huge, terrifying bear he built, which still stands in a cellar of the cursed commercial building.**

Neha the dicemaker explains that the taxidermist, Mr. Fabron, met his vision beast (an imaginary creature that guides you through life) while high on desiccants and named it Megatherion: 'a mega wild beast'.

*Real-world inspiration:* The name echoes Megatherium, an extinct giant ground sloth, whose name means 'great beast'.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

### Mesque

*Also: Northern Mesque, Mesques, mesques, Mesquese, Republic of Mesque*

**A huge nation on the isola of Mundi, the largest state in the world by territory. Many Mesque immigrants live in Revachol.**

Encyclopedia describes the Republic of Mesque as a confederation, a petrostate and a constitutional monarchy that has recently become an outcast for tilting to the far right. Joyce blames 'Mesque petrofascists' for spreading hateful language. Mesque is also one of the Coalition nations.

The steppes of Northern Mesque are home to the boiadeiro cow herders, and 'marietti' is a Mesque style of folk music and dance. 'Banger' means a Mesque gang member. Centuries ago Dolores Dei waged campaigns against the Mesque state when it tried to break away from innocentic rule.

*Real-world inspiration:* Loosely evokes Latin America, mixing Spanish and Portuguese words.

See also: [Magritte](#magritte), [Coalition](#coalition), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Mondial](#mondial)

### Messina

*Also: Messinian, Messinians*

**An Occidental nation and member of the Coalition. 'Messinian' means from Messina.**

Messina once formed the dual kingdom of Vesper-Messina on the southeastern coast of the Occident, before an amicable split into two republics. Vesper-Messina was the homeland of Dolores Dei, and Messina is remembered as the site of her crowning and a great centre of the Dolorian era. Its industry makes automated games, and instead of Fritttes it has Averro pharmacies.

The word 'isola' itself comes from Messinian.

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Frittte](#frittte), [Isola](#isola), [Mondial](#mondial), [Occident](#occident), [Vesper](#vesper)

### Meteo

*Also: Meteoran, Meteorans*

**A country on the isola of Mundi, by the Pisantic Sea. 'Meteoran' means from or of Meteo.**

Andre uses a Meteoran word for the Founding Party, the powerful religious organisation that owns the old church. Joyce mentions the plaza's restored Meteoran mosaic, and Measurehead counts the 'ancient Meteorans' in his race theory.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Mondial](#mondial)

### Mondial

*Also: Mondials*

**From Mundi, the largest isola. On police forms it is a broad racial category ('white-ish', says Encyclopedia), with subgroups such as Occidental.**

Kim records the hanged man's race as 'Mondial, subgroup Occidental'. The nations of Mundi (Mesque, the Occident and others) colonised Insulinde, and Joyce calls them the Mondials.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Mesque](#mesque), [Messina](#messina), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

### Mother of Silence

*Also: Church of the Mother of Silence*

**The silent, indescribable 'Mother' worshipped by Tiago, the blind man in the rafters of the old church. He calls the church her church.**

Tiago says she cannot be painted or sculpted, calling her a cavity in the dark, beyond sense. He promises that her love can lift you out of the despair at the bottom of the bottle, and says it burns away memory and selfish desire. Inland Empire warns that she sounds like a serious player.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Pale](#pale)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Tiago says the church was built around a 'perforation in the world' high up in its rafters, a way out into nothingness, which he circles and one day hopes to drink from.

</details>

### Motor carriage

*Also: motor carriages, motorcarriage*

**The in-world word for a car.**

See also: [Kineema](#kineema)

### Mundi

*Also: Mundi isola*

**The largest isola (continent) in the world, north of Insulinde across the pale; home of Oranje, Mesque and the other 'Occidental' nations.**

Joyce says the nations that colonised Insulinde called their own isola Mundi, 'the World', because it was all they knew. About three hundred years ago explorers from Mundi discovered Insulinde, and Mundi's nations went on to reach the other isolas in a great age of interisolary contact.

Encyclopedia places Oranje, Mesque and the Occidental sub-continent on Mundi, and Klaasje calls it the Old Old World. Dolores Dei ruled over both Mundi and Insulinde.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Oranje](#oranje), [Pale](#pale), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Corpus Mundi](#corpus-mundi)

### Nachtwey

**A gun maker. Its Nachtwey 80 (or A80) is an old-fashioned two-barrelled front-loading pistol.**

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Ruby owns a Nachtwey 80, and you can end up carrying it as your own sidearm.

</details>

### New New World

**A nickname for Insulinde, the isola Revachol stands on, discovered about three centuries ago by explorers who crossed the pale from Mundi.**

The discovery followed a series of costly, often deadly voyages into the pale, sponsored by Queen Irene La Navigateur on the advice of Dolores Dei. Joyce Messier describes the land they found as empty of people, home only to wildlife and the River Esperance, and says the nations of Mundi used it to amass the greatest concentration of wealth the world had ever seen: Revachol the Suzerain.

The opposite term, the Old Old World, means Mundi, where the colonists came from. Klaasje jokes that she came to Revachol to see the New New World, like a tourist.

*Real-world inspiration:* The 'New World' (the Americas), as Europeans called it in the age of exploration.

See also: [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Old Old World](#old-old-world), [Pale](#pale), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Revachol](#revachol)

### Nnong Okk

*Also: cursor owl*

**A flightless 'cursor owl' of the Semenine isles whose long legs supposedly make it the fastest-running bird, maybe the fastest animal.**

Morell mentions it among the cryptids he knows; he says the geologist Catelyn Mijanou also contributed to the search for it, though nobody remembers that.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Cryobacter katlensis](#cryobacter-katlensis)

### Occident

*Also: Occidental, Occidentals*

**A subcontinent of the Mundi isola, home of rich Western nations like Oranje; 'Occidental' is also used as a racial label for white people.**

Its nations include Oranje, Sur-la-Clef, Vesper and Messina, and six major Occidental nations hold a stake in the Coalition's occupation of Revachol. The Sunday Friend calls the Occident part of the 'normal world' and talks of a supranational alliance, the United States of Occident.

As a racial term, 'Occidental' means fair or light-skinned: on police forms it is a subgroup of the 'Mondial' race. It is also the word a racist lorry driver uses when he argues that white people should be in charge.

*Real-world inspiration:* The West (especially Western Europe); 'Occident' is an old word for the West.

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Messina](#messina), [New New World](#new-new-world), [Old Old World](#old-old-world), [Oranje](#oranje), [Sur-la-Clef](#sur-la-clef), [Vesper](#vesper)

### Old Old World

**A nickname for Mundi, the largest isola and home of the Occident, the old heartland from which the colonists of Insulinde came.**

It is the counterpart of the New New World, as people call Insulinde. Mundi is home to the wealthy Occidental nations such as Oranje, as well as other states like Mesque and Kedra. The nations that colonised Insulinde named their own isola Mundi, 'the World', because it was all they knew.

*Real-world inspiration:* The 'Old World' (Europe, Asia and Africa), as opposed to the Americas.

See also: [New New World](#new-new-world), [Occident](#occident), [Oranje](#oranje), [Insulinde](#insulinde), [Isola](#isola)

### Omicron

**The brand on Acele's tape recorder, a chunky, battery-powered machine in yellow plastic that she uses to record sounds.**

Acele got the recorder at the Paliseum, a music club where musicians loop and splice recorded sounds into music.

See also: [Paliseum](#paliseum)

### Oranje

*Also: Oranjese, Republic of Oranje, Sovereign Republic of Oranje*

**A democratic republic on the Mundi isola, seen as a 'reasonable superpower' and one of the six Coalition nations occupying Revachol.**

Oranje is a Moralintern nation and one of the most prominent members of the international community. Its people and things are called Oranjese, and its accent can be heard in Martinaise.

*Real-world inspiration:* Modelled on the Netherlands ('oranje' is Dutch for orange, the Dutch national colour).

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Klaasje](#klaasje)

### Pale

*Also: the Pale, the pale*

**The grey, featureless void that surrounds and separates the isolas (landmasses) of the world; exposure to it damages the mind.**

Pale covers most of the world's surface and is not really part of reality: the further in you go, the more the laws of physics break down, until even numbers stop working. It is colourless, odourless and hostile to matter and life. Its scientific study is called entroponetics.

People cross it in fast aerostatics (airships) to keep exposure short, and international rules strictly limit how many days a year civilians may spend in it, because it damages memory and the mind. It is measurably expanding at an unknown rate; talk of it one day covering everything is mostly left to extremists.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Elysium](#elysium), [Aerostatic](#aerostatic)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The 'swallow', a pillar of total silence inside Martinaise's old church, may be pale beginning to form inside the world itself rather than only at its edges.

</details>

### Pappa Lollo

**A popular brand of dairy products whose mascot smiles from milk cartons; it belongs to the powerful Looskap conglomerate.**

Encyclopedia lists it alongside the Bank of Consecration and Eeirberg as part of Looskap, a conglomerate that engineers financial disasters in 'second world' countries.

See also: [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It comes up when Klaasje tells her real story: she worked in corporate espionage, and a job involving a Looskap-owned savings bank has put the conglomerate on her trail.

</details>

### People's Republic of Samara

*Also: SRV, Samaran People's Republic*

**A communist state on the Samaran isola, founded as a socialist utopia by communist revolutionaries; now widely seen as a corrupt, degenerated rogue state.**

Encyclopedia calls it a product of Revachol's 'sister revolution' in Graad, established by survivors of the Revolution. It has since become a bureaucratic workers' state under the decades-long rule of President Sapormat 'Sport' Knezhinisky, known for brutal repression, monumental corruption and an obsession with physical fitness. Ignus Nilsen, Kras Mazov's lieutenant, is honoured there as a founding father.

It sends 'solidarity aid' abroad, which is why some of the cheap goods in Martinaise, like a motor skiff and a pair of speakers, carry its stamp.

See also: [Communism](#communism), [Ignus Nilsen](#ignus-nilsen), [Isola](#isola), [Kras Mazov](#kras-mazov), [President Knezhinisky](#president-knezhinisky), [Revolution](#revolution), [ROO A72 Motor Skiff](#roo-a72-motor-skiff), [Sam Bo](#sam-bo), [Samara](#samara)

### Perikarnassian Church

*Also: Perikarnassianism, Perikarnassian theology, Perikarnassian Theory of Love, Star of Perikarnassis*

**An ancient church whose central symbol, the Star of Perikarnassis, stands for a great moral height to strive towards.**

The star symbol is also called the Chi-Rho. The Founding Party, the world's oldest international organisation, is described as the church's custodian and as the body that anoints the innocences.

The Perikarnassian Theory of Love holds that love is a relay out of death: it lives on, generation after generation, in our children. Some theorists even argue that communism is a secular version of Perikarnassian theology, swapping faith in the divine for faith in humanity's future. The speedfreaks camped by the old abandoned church insist that it is all about love, just like their music.

*Real-world inspiration:* Christianity: the Chi-Rho is a real early Christian symbol.

See also: [The Perikarnassian](#the-perikarnassian), [Innocence](#innocence), [Dolorian](#dolorian), [Anodic music](#anodic-music)

### Perseus Black

**A recognisable clothing label: the RCM's signature uniforms are Perseus Black, and a new Perseus Black outfit turns heads on the street.**

Esprit de Corps notes that some officers wear the uniform all the way to the top ranks of the RCM and are buried in it, while others, like you, wear it more casually.

See also: [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Savoir Faire](#savoir-faire)

### Phasmid Trap

*Also: Phasmid traps, Trap: Land's End, Trap: Boathouses, Trap: Canal, Trap: Morell*

**One of Morell's mesh cylinders, baited with live locusts and hidden in the coastal reeds to catch the Insulindian Phasmid.**

Lena designed the traps: the phasmid is supposed to crawl down a funnel to eat the locusts and then be unable to climb back out. The netting is soft so it won't damage the insect's delicate shell. Morell has placed them in the reeds at Land's End, near the boathouses and by the canal, and you can check them for him.

See also: [Morell](#morell), [Lena](#lena), [Insulindian phasmid](#insulindian-phasmid)

### Poste L'Aventurier

*Also: L'Aventurier*

**A postal company; one of its mail collection boxes stands in Martinaise, covered in graffiti and pierced by two bullet holes.**

Call Me Mañana says the company once tried to recruit him as a postman, promising the job was fun and easy, just walking. He did not believe them.

See also: [Martinaise](#martinaise)

### Radiocomputer

*Also: radiocomputers*

**The in-world word for a computer: a bulky machine that works over radio.**

See also: [Interfacing](#interfacing)

### Rehm

*Also: Rehm Civic, Rehm Prefect, Rehm Prefects, Rehm Rational*

**A leading maker of radiocomputers. The Civic is its everyday model, the Prefect the government version and the Rational a military-grade series.**

Soona, the programmer camped in the abandoned church, works on a Rehm Prefect (model RC7024, with a Feld mainframe and a Rehm-compatible Interim printer). Encyclopedia explains it is built on the same technology as the Civic, with better noise filtering; Soona says it also works better with newer antennas, and that the Civic is widely agreed to be below all standards. Another Rehm Civic (model RC5120) sits in an abandoned office in the Doomed Commercial Area.

Trant Heidelstam counts Rehm among today's tech giants, and the Mega Rich Light-Bending Guy can't wait for its next model.

See also: [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer), [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

If you bring Soona the transceiver from the Doomed Commercial Area's radiocomputer for your attempt to radio Coalition Warship Archer, she recognises it as the one from her own old Rehm Civic, and says it will do.

</details>

### reál

*Also: reáls, IIR, interisolary reál*

**The reál (IIR, or interisolary reál): the world's reserve currency, and the money you earn, owe and spend in Revachol.**

Encyclopedia explains that the interisolary reál is the global reserve currency, so it is used wherever you go. Its paper notes are black and carry the faces of past Innocences; the Innocence Franconegro is credited with making it the world's currency.

You begin the game short of money and owing Garte for your room at the Whirling-in-Rags. For scale, an RCM officer in this region earns about 5,500 reál a year.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Garte](#garte), [Innocence](#innocence)

### Reál Belt

**The wide group of nations under the Moralintern's influence; Klaasje calls Revachol its most volatile corner.**

Kim explains that the Moralintern is a union of centre-left and centre-right parties across the Reál Belt, with the Coalition government of Revachol as just one of its projects. Its policies apply in all of these nations: Encyclopedia notes, for instance, that peacetime police are restricted to front-loading rifles. Their legal systems are built to accommodate rule by an innocence, should one arise, and in their cultures the lungs are the symbol of love.

Kim scoffs at paranoid types who believe the Moralintern keeps score sheets on everyone in the Reál Belt.

See also: [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Coalition](#coalition), [Innocence](#innocence), [reál](#reál)

### ROO A72 Motor Skiff

*Also: ROO A72*

**A small, battered two-seat motor boat in the fishing village; Lilienne the net picker can lend it to you to cross the bay.**

Encyclopedia identifies it as humanitarian aid from the People's Republic of Samara: the ROO logo and a white five-pointed star on the engine give it away. Lilienne has just tarred it and warns that it is strictly a no-rocking boat.

See also: [Fishing village](#fishing-village), [People's Republic of Samara](#peoples-republic-of-samara), [Samara](#samara)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It takes you out to the old sea fortress on the island in the bay, and back again.

</details>

### Safre

*Also: Safre Empire, South Safre*

**A former empire, once forced by Revachol's kings into the cocaine trade; South Safre is now the scene of a long proxy war between Graad and Safre.**

Your Encyclopedia notes that in the last century Revachol's suzerain flooded Safre with cocaine under an exclusive contract, creating a captive market; half of the former Safre Empire still depends on foreign aid. Safre also had its own short-lived uprising during the world revolution, crushed in '06.

South Safre, on the Samaran isola, is a hub of bulk chemical manufacturing and the site of the South Safre conflict: a proxy war with Graad, twelve years hot, with atrocities committed mostly by Graad. The 'Suzerainty' board game re-enacts the cocaine episode.

*Real-world inspiration:* Imperial China and the Opium Wars; the South Safre conflict echoes the Vietnam War.

See also: [Graad](#graad), [Isola](#isola)

### Saint-Batiste Pharmaceutics

*Also: Saint-Batiste Pharmaceuticals*

**A Revacholian pharmaceutical company whose logo is on the medicines in the Frittte's cabinet and the Whirling's medicine cabinet.**

Named after the city of Saint-Batiste, it makes everyday remedies such as nosaphed, drouamine, magnesium and hypnogamma. Encyclopedia lists Saint-Batiste among the old indotribes, companies granted royal monopolies centuries ago, and Joyce lists it among the clients of the mercenaries' security company.

Your skills disagree about it: Authority calls it a narco outfit luring people into the gutter, while Inland Empire swells with pride at a proud local company.

See also: [Saint-Batiste](#saint-batiste), [Frittte](#frittte), [Wild Pines](#wild-pines), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries)

### Samara

*Also: Samaran, Samaran isola, South Samara, South Samaran, South-Samaran*

**One of the great isolas (continent-sized landmasses) of Elysium, lying to the east; 'Samaran' describes its people, goods and culture.**

It is home to the communist People's Republic of Samara (SRV), founded by survivors of the Revolution and now widely seen as a degenerated rogue state, and to places such as Siigay, South Safre, Hsin-Yao and the Koko Nur desert. Many of its nations lie outside the Moralintern's umbrella.

Samaran things are all over Martinaise: goat-milk butter, boxing (Sam Bo), folk songs, cheap 'solidarity aid' goods, and people such as the washerwoman, who was born there, and the street vendor in the traffic jam.

See also: [Isola](#isola), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [People's Republic of Samara](#peoples-republic-of-samara), [Sam Bo](#sam-bo), [Samaran Butter](#samaran-butter), [Semper Maa](#semper-maa), [Seol](#seol), [Siigay](#siigay)

### Sansarique

**A sportswear brand with a V-shaped logo that makes 'premium' tracksuits and running shoes.**

The down-and-out former creative director at the fishing village fondly remembers how unstoppable he felt in his Sansarique Lickra tracksuit.

See also: [Lickra](#lickra)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Very late in the case, the old man you finally confront wears worn-out Sansarique running shoes (model Corbeille, size 43) with a black and white spiral pattern on the soles.

</details>

### Saramiriza

*Also: Saramirizian, Saramirizians*

**A distant land known for cocaine, coffee and crisp white twill jackets; 'Saramirizian' means from there.**

On the Suzerainty board game, Supramundi and Saramiriza supply magenta cocaine leaves, and the Paledriver hints at what she hauls on her trips from Saramiriza to Graad. Evrart Claire lists it among the proxy-war zones where hardened mercenaries learned their trade.

A classic white Saramirizian jacket is a recognisable style: Steban says only old Saramirizian communists and drug smugglers still wear them. Shivers even pictures a Saramirizian restaurant in Revachol that serves homemade wine.

See also: [Supramundi](#supramundi), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries)

### Semenese ward

**A traditional charm from Semenine, meant to protect against bad luck, bad dreams, curses and other supernatural threats.**

Plaisance, who runs the bookstore in the Doomed Commercial Area, has hung dozens of them over the curtained back door of her shop to keep the 'doom' of the place at bay; a pair of Semenese mediators supplied them. She also wears a pendant she trusts to protect her. Soona, the programmer in the church, once found her terminal covered in similar wards.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

### Semenine

*Also: Semenese, Semenine Islands, Semenine islands, Semenine Isles, Semenine isles, Semenese islands, Semenese Islands, Free State of Semenine, South Semenese*

**A remote southern archipelago near the pale, also called Ile du Fantôme; 'Semenese' means its people and culture.**

The bookstore's wall map places the Semenese islands far to the southwest of Revachol, across the Insulindic ocean. The country is called the Free State of Semenine. Encyclopedia calls it a beautiful place with a rich and unique culture; Inland Empire prefers to imagine mist and wild shamans. Plaisance, the bookseller, covers her doors in Semenese wards against her shop's curse.

The islands recently suffered a proxy war in which mercenaries fought for Oranjese pharmaceutical interests. Measurehead proudly calls himself Semenese, claims the Semenese are the true indigenous people of the Insulindian archipelago and preaches Semenese supremacy, and the slur 'kipt' targets people of Semenese descent.

See also: [Measurehead](#measurehead), [Oranje](#oranje), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [Pale](#pale)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The hanged man's tattoos show he served as a mercenary in the Semenine conflict.

</details>

### Semper Maa

**A cryptid said to live in Samara's subtropical jungles: a 'colonial animal' that looks like mud and can supposedly reverse time.**

Gary, the cryptofascist, describes it as a mass of tiny organisms acting as one body, almost impossible to spot or catch, and very dangerous. When threatened it is said to revert to an earlier stage of its life cycle, perhaps endlessly, and the reversal supposedly turns back time for anything touching it.

Gary recommends it when you ask him about ways to turn back time.

*Real-world inspiration:* The 'immortal jellyfish' (Turritopsis dohrnii), which can revert to an earlier stage of its life cycle.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Samara](#samara)

### Seol

*Also: Seolite, Seolites*

**A distant nation and culture. Kim Kitsuragi is of Seolite descent.**

Seolites are a minority in Revachol and face prejudice there; several characters make racist remarks about Kim.

*Real-world inspiration:* Seolite culture is coded as East Asian.

See also: [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

### Seraise

*Also: Seraise Empire*

**An empire on the Samaran isola with its own language. Cheap plastic, rice farmers and a famously deadly giant hornet all come from there.**

Its provinces include the Siigay archipelago, nicknamed the 'Apricot Suzerainty' in Revachol (a term Kim calls fraught). In Lo Manthang, young religious protesters have been setting themselves on fire to oppose the Seraise government and creeping Occidental influence. Other mentions include the poet Lu Jiatun and the 'Vulgate Seraise' language.

See also: [Seraise Giant Hornet](#seraise-giant-hornet), [Occident](#occident)

### Seraise Giant Hornet

*Also: Giant Seraise Hornet*

**The world's second-largest insect: a hornet that can kill forty honey bees a minute. A small group can wipe out a whole hive in hours.**

Your Encyclopedia skill offers up this fact while you talk with Joyce, and you can later use it yourself as a grim comparison for what a small, determined group can do.

*Real-world inspiration:* The Asian giant hornet, notorious for massacring honeybee hives.

See also: [Seraise](#seraise)

### Siigay

*Also: Apricot Suzerainty, Siigayan*

**An archipelago on the Samaran isola, once colonised by Revachol; Revacholians still call it the 'Apricot Suzerainty', a name with a sting.**

Revachol once colonised Siigay, so the nickname 'Apricot Suzerainty' counts as a slur. Today it is described as a province of the Seraise Empire, and Samaran factories there ship goods through the Martinaise harbour.

Siileng, a street vendor in Martinaise, grew up there and is blunt about why he left. Encyclopedia notes that Siigayan apricots are processed in Sur-la-Clef into apricot-flavoured chewing gum.

See also: [Apricot Chewing Gum Wrapper](#apricot-chewing-gum-wrapper), [Harbour](#harbour), [Samara](#samara), [Siileng](#siileng), [Suzerain](#suzerain), [Tutti Frutti](#tutti-frutti)

### Slipstream

*Also: Slipstream SCA*

**A failed company from the Doomed Commercial Area that made rotor blades, then skis, before going under.**

Its logo is on the skis and rotor blades left lying around in an abandoned building. According to the Novelty Dicemaker, the company 'pivoted' from blades to skis, then its chief executive went on a 'vacation' with all the money, and bailiffs seized what was left in '47.

Its name is still on the building's old doorbell panel, next to the other businesses that failed.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Pressing the Slipstream SCA doorbell gets a strange answer: a woman's voice introducing herself as Tricentennial Electrics. It may be a recording a hundred years old.

</details>

### Sola

*Also: Solas*

**An outspoken urban planner who became an Innocence last century, often called the 'anti-innocence'. Banknotes with her face are nicknamed Solas.**

She was anointed in the previous century and lived into the current one. Instead of offering herself as a model for history, she encouraged people to do well on their own.

She resigned after a Yugo nationalist tried to assassinate her, blaming her for not siding with the left during the turn-of-the-century revolutions. Innocences don't usually resign.

Black paper banknotes carry the faces of Innocences, which is why Savoir Faire calls cash 'Franconegros and Solas'.

See also: [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Ernö Pasternak](#ernö-pasternak), [Innocence](#innocence), [reál](#reál)

### Stas-Rajko

*Also: Stas-Rajko KK-2*

**A classic, tractor-tough motor carriage made in Zsiemsk. One painted a muddy brown is parked in Martinaise.**

Encyclopedia rates the KK-2 model as one of the finest Zsiemsk-made motor carriages. Its emblem is the Eight Antler, and every Stas-Rajko comes baby-blue by default: the blue-and-white colours of the ancient Zsiemsk ruler Zygismunt the Great.

Kim remembers a case from his precinct in which Zsiemsk migrants killed the driver of a repainted Stas-Rajko and called it an honour killing.

See also: [Motor carriage](#motor-carriage), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi), [Precinct 57](#precinct-57)

### Supramundi

**A far-off land counted among the nations of Mundi; it turns up as a source of cocaine and a corridor for brutal proxy wars.**

Encyclopedia says even far-away Supramundi accepted the rule of the innocence Dolores Dei. Today Joyce Messier lists it, with Yeesut and the Semenese islands, among the conflict zones where atrocities go unreported, and on the Suzerainty board game it supplies magenta cocaine along with Saramiriza. Its dust storms reach as far as Kedra.

See also: [Saramiriza](#saramiriza), [Semenine](#semenine), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei)

### Sur-la-Clef

**A rich, modern Occidental nation and Coalition member. Much of Revachol's language and culture comes from it, and it hosts the main EPIS institutions.**

Its predecessor, the Kingdom of Suresne, once owned Revachol as a colony, which is why Revachol's language (Suresne), many of its people and some of its food come from there. Queen Irene La Navigateur of Suresne sponsored the costly voyages into the pale that discovered the isola you are standing on.

Today Sur-la-Clef is a highly developed, urbanised country, best known as the business and executive heart of EPIS, the programme the Moralintern created to support certain Occidental nations. The Coalition's Institute of Price Stabilité is based there too.

See also: [Occident](#occident), [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Revachol](#revachol), [Oranje](#oranje)

### Suresne

*Also: Kingdom of Suresne*

**The old kingdom that first colonised Revachol, a forerunner of today's Sur-la-Clef. Its name lives on as the language Revacholians speak.**

In the earliest colonial days the Kingdom of Suresne owned the city of Revachol. Its queen, Irene La Navigateur, sent eight expeditions into the pale, and Dolores Dei was her principal advisor.

Kim points out that there is no 'Revacholian language': people in Revachol speak Suresne.

See also: [Revachol](#revachol), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Pale](#pale), [Suzerain](#suzerain)

### Suru

*Also: Suruese*

**An indigenous, often red-haired minority from Vaasa on the cold isola of Katla. Revachol has a small Suruese community.**

Encyclopedia places their homeland far away in Katla, beyond 'winter's orbit', a freezing land where a day is said to last 25 hours. When Cunoesse sings what Cuno calls the song of her people, Encyclopedia recognises it as Suruese, and adds that the Suruese carry a 'ginger gene'.

See also: [Cunoesse](#cunoesse), [Cuno](#cuno)

### Touloula

**A cheap vodka brand. Empty 'Touloula' bottles turn up on the Martinaise boardwalk.**

The name also comes up as a far-off place: the Novelty Dicemaker imagines Slipstream's runaway boss sending holiday messages from Touloula, Tioumoutiri or Khasht-Kor.

See also: [Slipstream](#slipstream)

### Tricentennial Electrics

*Also: Tricentennial*

**A major electric company of about a century ago; a woman claiming to work there answers one of the doorbells of the Doomed Commercial Area.**

It was one of the fourteen indotribes, the old Revacholian companies granted royal monopolies by the king, and it once beat Feld in business machines. The Novelty Dicemaker only remembers it as a big firm from a hundred years ago.

When you press the button for Slipstream SCA on the building's old call box, a fragile voice full of static answers as Tricentennial Electrics and asks if you have come to place an order.

See also: [Doomed Commercial Area](#doomed-commercial-area)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The voice seems to mistake you for someone called Michel who left her months ago, and on a second call it repeats itself word for word, like an ancient recording. Kim's best guess is an electrical anomaly.

</details>

### Tutti Frutti

**A brand of apricot-flavoured chewing gum. You find one of its wrappers in a jeans pocket, and the smell stirs something painful.**

The worn wrapper smells faintly of apricots and cinnamon. Encyclopedia notes that the apricots probably come from Siigay and are made into gum in Sur-la-Clef. Your skills urge you to throw the wrapper away before it hurts you.

See also: [Siigay](#siigay)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The scent is tied to the woman Harry lost, and it comes back in his memories and dreams of her.

</details>

### Ubi Sunt?

*Also: Ubi, Ubis*

**Poor, crumbling isles at the edge of the Mundi isola, breaking apart into the pale. Their people, the Ubi, are known for socialism and sheep.**

Encyclopedia says it is the only place in the world with a question mark in its name. The game calls it an island, a group of isles and a 'vanishing peninsula' on the Mundi isola; all agree it is a place of poverty and entroponetic collapse at the edge of the pale.

Ubi herdsmen came to Insulinde on the first boats and brought their own flowery folk style of Dolorian architecture. Ubi accents are thick, Conceptualization links an archaic-sounding quote to Ubi dialect, and Kim recognises Ubi folk dancing when he sees it. Easy Leo in the container yard is Ubi and insists the sheep rumours are lies; Lilienne the net picker is half Ubi through her mother.

*Real-world inspiration:* 'Ubi sunt?' is Latin for 'Where are they?', the opening of a classic medieval lament for things lost to time.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Dolorian](#dolorian), [Pale](#pale), [Ubi Dolorianism](#ubi-dolorianism), [Vesper](#vesper)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Noid believes that Ubi herdsmen, who came over on the first boats, built the old church on the coast centuries ago to contain something.

</details>

### ULAN frequency

*Also: ULAN, ULAN frequencies, Ulan frequencies, ULAN frequency system*

**An obscure radio frequency system described in notes found in an abandoned lorry cabin. Even Kim has never heard of it.**

The notes are an article torn out of a radio enthusiasts' magazine, full of complex equations. Kim knows FM, AM and UKV, but not ULAN; he guesses it could be a way to link fleets without interference, or a way to tap into RCM networks.

See also: [Pale](#pale)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The lorry belongs to Ruby, who dabbles in advanced radio theory. When you finally track her down, she attacks your mind with a pale emitter blasting the ULAN frequency.

</details>

### Vaasa

*Also: Vaasan, Vaasans, Vaasa City*

**A wealthy social democratic nation on the cold isola of Katla, known (says your Encyclopedia) for impossibly blonde people.**

Vaasa is called a social democratic powerhouse. Its social democrats share many of the Moralintern's values yet refuse to join Moralism. The communist revolutionary Ignus Nilsen was Vaasan, and Trant Heidelstam recognises 'Ask the Wind' as a Vaasan lullaby.

Morell the cryptozoologist cites centuries of cryptid sightings in Vaasa's forests.

*Real-world inspiration:* The Nordic countries and their social democracies.

See also: [Katla](#katla), [Moralism](#moralism), [Moralintern](#moralintern)

### Vermillion

*Also: Vermillion Golden Spirits, Vermillion spirits, Vermillon*

**A local brewery whose 'Golden Spirits' logo is printed on the yellow cargo belt the victim was hanged with. Dockworkers use the same belts in the harbour.**

Kim records the belt in the autopsy: yellow, three metres long, 'Vermillion spirits' printed on the side. Titus Hardie recognises it at once as the kind of belt they use in the harbour, and you can find identical belts there.

The brewery's beer turns up elsewhere too: a six-pack of 'Vermillon Roi d'Or' (spelled that way) comes with a free plastic crown stamped ROI D'OR.

See also: [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Harbour](#harbour), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie)

### Vesper

*Also: Republic of Vesper, Vesper-Messina, Vespertine, Vespertines*

**A wealthy Occidental nation and Coalition member, known for its exotic financial products. 'Vespertine' means from Vesper.**

Vesper was once joined with Messina as the dual kingdom of Vesper-Messina (Dolores Dei's homeland) before the two parted amicably; Vesper is the richer half. It sits on the edge of the Mundi isola, close to the pale and overlooking Ubi Sunt?, and has a reputation for an almost ominous stability.

Vespertine culture is everywhere in Revachol: the hero of the Dick Mullen crime novels is a Vespertine detective, Kim says RCM fashions borrow from classic Vespertine cop shows, and people mention Vespertine spy novels, rock stars and art movements. Joyce Messier holds Vespertine citizenship through her husband.

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Messina](#messina), [Occident](#occident), [Pale](#pale), [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Ubi Sunt?](#ubi-sunt)

### Weiss-Wiesemann coefficient

*Also: Weiss-Wiesemann*

**A measure of wealth inequality between people: near 1, one person owns everything. Past about .96, the laws of physics reportedly bend around the rich.**

Encyclopedia explains it when you notice that the light behaves strangely around the Mega Rich Light-Bending Guy in the container yard. The coefficient between you and him works out to .9998 repeating. He has heard of the effect, though naturally he has never witnessed it himself.

*Real-world inspiration:* The Gini coefficient, the real-world measure of income or wealth inequality.

See also: [Ultraliberalism](#ultraliberalism), [Plutocracy](#plutocracy)

### Western Plain

**An ancient name for the pale: Joyce Messier says the Perikarnassians of thousands of years ago called it the Western Plain.**

Joyce Messier says people have studied the pale for 6,000 years, and the Perikarnassians called it the Western Plain; there are signs of crossings even in pre-modern times.

The Paledriver in Jamrock names it among the pale routes she has driven, and recommends an old ballad, 'On the Western Plain', about a daring boiadeiro who promises to marry a girl when he returns from the plain. He comes back a changed man, kills her by the River Magritte and rides off again.

See also: [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Pale](#pale), [Paledriver](#paledriver)

### Willow People

*Also: willow person*

**A cryptid Morell nearly caught: very thin, almost flat beings that hide by wrapping themselves around trees and blending into the bark.**

Morell compares them to the phasmid he is hunting in Martinaise. He and Gary once painted a whole grove of trees bright lavender with slow-drying paint, hoping to expose one, and after hours of waiting he glimpsed a lavender shadow dashing away.

See also: [Cryptid](#cryptid), [Cryptozoology](#cryptozoology), [Insulindian phasmid](#insulindian-phasmid)

### Yeesut

**A distant country known mainly for war and famine: one of the 'conflict corridors' where mercenaries fight proxy wars.**

Joyce Messier lists it with Supramundi and the Semenese islands as places with a poor record of reporting military atrocities, and Evrart Claire describes the company mercenaries as killers from proxy wars in Yeesut, Semenine and Saramiriza. Klaasje mentions the '36 famine in Yeesut. Measurehead blames the 'al-chemists of Yeesut and Bashir' for inventing Al Gul.

See also: [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [Al Gul](#al-gul), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier)

### Yugo-Graad

*Also: Yugo-Graadian*

**A region of Graad known for ethnic unrest, vodka and the Yugo-Graad Riots of '27 to '29.**

Your Encyclopedia describes the Yugo-Graad Riots as a wave of ethnic unrest met by state repression, which ended a short period of liberal reform known as the Yugo-Graad Spring. The bleak romance novel 'Sixteen Days of Coldest April' is set during the riots.

Yugo-Graadians are one of the Kojko peoples of Graad, and even a racist lorry driver admits their vodka is good.

See also: [Graad](#graad), [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Klaasje's real name turns out to be Graadian, Yugo-Graad or Zsiemsk in origin.

</details>

### Zsiemsk

*Also: Zsiemsk-made*

**A people and region of Graad, known in Revachol for its migrants, its sturdy Stas-Rajko motor carriages and its ancient kings.**

Visual Calculus counts the Zsiemsk among the Kojkos of Graad, along with the Yugo and Shest. Encyclopedia praises the Zsiemsk-made Stas-Rajko, built like a tractor and used as one in the countryside, in the zaffer blue and white of Zygismunt the Great. Hand/Eye Coordination knows the Ister 50 as a Zsiemsk-made monstrosity of a gun.

Kim remembers an old case: Zsiemsk migrants murdered the driver of a Stas-Rajko painted muddy brown, calling it an honour killing, and five thousand people protested the trial.

See also: [Zygismunt the Great](#zygismunt-the-great), [Motor carriage](#motor-carriage), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Klaasje eventually admits that her parents were Zsiemsk immigrants, though she grew up in Oranje.

</details>

## Factions

### Barmy Army

**A local Martinaise gang that Titus Hardie boasts the Hardie Boys have already seen off, along with the 'Madre scum'.**

It comes up when you warn the Hardies about the mercenaries. Kim points out that trained military professionals are not a street gang or a Barmy Army; Titus answers that the local gangs were tough too, and look where they are now.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries)

### Census Bureau

**The government's population records office; Evrart Claire claims his folder of information about you came from there.**

When you notice that the folder on Evrart's desk carries no RCM stamp, he cheerfully admits it is from the Census Bureau and jokes that the people there are 'absolutely corrupt'. Kim reckons Evrart got only your name from the bureau, and everything else from watching what you have been doing in Martinaise.

See also: [Evrart Claire](#evrart-claire)

### Coalition

*Also: Coalition of Nations, the Coalition, Coalition Government*

**The alliance of wealthy nations that crushed Revachol's revolution and now governs the city.**

About fifty years ago the Coalition landed in Revachol and crushed the communist Commune. Since then the city has been an occupied 'Zone of Control' with almost no government of its own, run under Coalition rule and guided by the Moralintern.

Joyce Messier names its members as Graad, Mesque, Vesper, Messina, Oranje and Sur-La-Clef.

*Real-world inspiration:* Foreign powers that intervene in other countries' revolutions, and international institutions that manage struggling economies.

See also: [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Moralism](#moralism), [Revolution](#revolution), [Commune](#commune), [Revachol](#revachol)

### Committee of Responsibility

*Also: Committee of Responsibility for Revachol, Comité de Responsabilité de Revachol*

**A Coalition body that, the Sunday Friend explains, acts as a clearinghouse for Coalition activities and the final arbiter of 'la responsabilité' in Revachol.**

It comes up during a Moralist side quest, in which you set out to form a committee that can assign 'la responsabilité'. The Coalition official known as the Sunday Friend is delighted to tell you there is no need: such a committee already exists, though he cannot connect you to it himself.

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Moralism](#moralism)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

You can reach it through a radio call with the Coalition Warship Archer. The Committee accepts your request and sends the Coalition Shuttle Laurel to extract you; if you climb aboard, you abandon the case and the game ends, with newspapers later reporting no answers about the extracted cop.

</details>

### Constabulary

*Also: Constabulatory*

**The RCM's upper administration, which decides on things like equipment; the game also spells it 'Constabulatory'.**

Kim says the RCM has asked for years for armour like the dead man's for its tactical units, but the Constabulary deemed it too costly, and six officers have been lost to semi-automatics since. Harry later picks up the word and suggests taking matters into your own hands, and Judit Minot warns that the Constabulatory will not accept wild speculation in a case.

See also: [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi), [Judit Minot](#judit-minot)

### Corps de Pharmacie

**A private security firm hired to guard Oranjese pharmaceutical interests in the Semenine conflict; it has been renamed several times since.**

See also: [Oranje](#oranje), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

When you have the dispatcher run the serial number from the hanged man's boot, the armour turns out to be part of a special order for this firm. The paper trail ends in Semenine.

</details>

### Emergencies Desk

*Also: RCM Emergencies Desk*

**The RCM's emergency phone line. Its number, 8-100-2, is printed on faded stickers all over Martinaise.**

The case began when an anonymous caller told the Emergencies Desk there was a dead body behind the Whirling-in-Rags. The stickers also carry the slogan 'Mankind, be vigilant!'

See also: [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Klaasje eventually says she was the one who phoned in the body, using the number from those stickers.

</details>

### Emergency Relief Brigade

**A self-organised volunteer force that helped fire brigades contain the radioactive spill from the People's Pile disaster. Bird's Nest Roy says he served in it.**

The People's Pile was a primitive nuclear reactor that failed right after it was switched on, leaking radioactive waste into the River Esperance. Roy still wears the brigade's orange safety jacket with a white triangle, and says the volunteers spent years in the river mud and many fell ill. He says that is why he takes the anti-radiation drug Pyrholidon.

*Real-world inspiration:* The Chernobyl liquidators who cleaned up after the 1986 nuclear disaster.

See also: [Faubourg](#faubourg)

### Founding Party

*Also: Ecclesiastes, Holy Party*

**The world's oldest international organisation: a powerful religious body that elects the rare Innocences. It owns the abandoned church in Martinaise.**

In the days of the Perikarnassian it was called the Holy Party, and it is thought to have begun with the first innocence's disciples. Electing an innocence to office has happened only six times in history.

After Dolores Dei's assassination, many of the members who had elected her founded the Moralintern to carry on her project; others moved to Sur-la-Clef and invented modern corporate finance. Andre's crew uses its Meteoran name, the Ecclesiastes, and says it owns the abandoned church they want to turn into a club.

See also: [Dolorian Church of Humanity](#dolorian-church-of-humanity), [Dolores Dei](#dolores-dei), [Dolorian](#dolorian), [Innocence](#innocence), [Moralintern](#moralintern)

### Hardie boys

*Also: Hardie boy, Hardies*

**A gang of union dockworkers led by Titus Hardie who act as Martinaise's self-appointed enforcers.**

Kim hears them described as the Union's armed wing: the men who 'keep the peace' in Martinaise. They drink in the Whirling-in-Rags cafeteria and openly claim responsibility for the hanging, insisting it was justice.

*Real-world inspiration:* The name riffs on 'The Hardy Boys', a long-running series of boy-detective novels.

See also: [Titus Hardie](#titus-hardie), [Union](#union), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Their story about the hanging is a cover for what really happened.

</details>

### Humanox

**A well-known non-profit humanitarian aid organisation; one of its lorries stands in the traffic jam by the harbour with its seals broken.**

Encyclopedia says Humanox was set up by EPIS countries (a Moralintern programme) to help during humanitarian crises. Logic notices the broken seals and concludes that someone is stealing from it.

See also: [Moralintern](#moralintern)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The lorry belongs to the street vendor by the traffic jam, who is selling its cargo at his stall; the racist lorry driver is happy to tell you so.

</details>

### Inspectorate General

*Also: rat squad*

**The RCM's internal affairs office, which investigates police officers themselves; cops call it the 'rat squad'.**

Kim mentions it when you first meet: he could suspect you of impersonating an officer, but he isn't from the Inspectorate General, so he sets those doubts aside.

See also: [Revachol Citizens Militia](#revachol-citizens-militia), [Kim Kitsuragi](#kim-kitsuragi)

### Institute of Price Stabilité

*Also: Institute of Price Stability*

**A Moralintern institute in Sur-la-Clef devoted to keeping inflation just below 2%; a Coalition official you can meet works for it.**

Its commissioner, the polished Sunday Friend, explains that price stability is the heart of sound monetary policy and one of the Moralintern's main projects, and offers you an informational pamphlet. Encyclopedia suspects it's a joke, but Trant Heidelstam confirms it exists and works with the Coalition's Bank of the World mission in Revachol.

See also: [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralism](#moralism)

### INSURCOM

*Also: Coalition Government Insulindian Mission Command, Coalition Government Insulindian Mission Control*

**The Coalition's military command for Insulinde, in charge of the occupation of Revachol; its offices fill the lower floors of the Bank of the World skyscraper.**

Trant Heidelstam explains that during the Revolution INSURCOM coordinated the Occidental and Graadian invasion, but its role today is mostly logistical. It is one of the spokes radiating from the Comité de Responsabilité, the hub of Coalition affairs in Revachol.

See also: [Coalition](#coalition), [Moralintern](#moralintern), [Revolution](#revolution), [Aerostatic](#aerostatic)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Coalition Warship Archer, the aerostatic you may eventually contact, is the flagship of INSURCOM forces in Revachol.

</details>

### Internal Affairs

**The office that investigates police officers' own wrongdoing; Kim warns that drawing its attention is a very bad idea.**
