The game slowly reveals that his life collapsed after his fiancée left him several years ago, and that he has been drinking himself toward death ever since. He was once considered an exceptional detective.

</details>

### Iosef Lilianovich Dros

*Also: Dros, the Deserter, Iosef, Lilianovich*

**An old man you may meet very late in the case.**

See also: [Revolution](#revolution), [Commune](#commune), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Dros is the killer. He is a deserter from the communist army who has hidden alone on a ruined island fort since the Revolution was crushed, about fifty years ago. He shot Lely from the island out of envy and bitterness.

</details>

### Kortenaer

*Also: Korty, Raul Kortenaer, Sergeant Major Raul Kortenaer*

**A hot-tempered Krenel mercenary nicknamed 'Korty', one of the three company mercenaries that Joyce Messier says arrived in Martinaise.**

Joyce describes Korty as the unit's gunner, working alongside the radio operator Phillis de Paule; the mercenaries come from Krenel, an Oranjese military company, and report to her only sporadically. He boasts of being a veteran officer with more than fifteen years of combat behind him.

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Krenel](#krenel), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He is the huge, ill-disguised 'scab leader' preaching the Right to Work at the harbour gates. The hanged man was his commander; the two grew up together on the same farm. Drunk and grieving, he leads an armed mercenary 'tribunal' outside the Whirling-in-Rags to avenge him.

</details>

### Lely

*Also: Ellis Kortenaer*

**The service name (nom de guerre) the hanged man went by.**

See also: [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Lely was the commander of the mercenaries hired to protect the company's interests in the strike, and Klaasje's lover.

</details>

### Loosduin County Savings Bank

**A small-sounding bank that actually belongs to the giant Looskap conglomerate; it comes up in Klaasje's past.**

See also: [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Klaasje admits it was the target of the second corporate-espionage job she did, and that many ordinary people lost their jobs because of it. Looskap and the people who hired her are now after her.

</details>

### Mega Rich Light-Bending Guy

*Also: Mr. Diodore, Roustame Diodore*

**A staggeringly rich investor hiding inside a shipping container in the harbour's container yard; his wealth literally bends the light around him.**

He introduces himself as Roustame Diodore, an investor and 'extremely high-net-worth individual' who inherited his fortune and travels in the container to avoid crowds and advertising. You can't make out his features, because the light swims around him; Encyclopedia blames the extreme wealth gap between you.

He is friendly in a breezy ultraliberal way, lectures on markets and modernism, and listens to investment pitches. If you have ideas, he may stake you a little seed money.

See also: [Harbour](#harbour), [reál](#reál), [Rehm](#rehm), [Ultraliberalism](#ultraliberalism)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Evrart Claire laughs off the idea of light-bending millionaires in his containers, and Kim insists the man bent no light at all: that part was in your head.

</details>

### Mercenary tribunal

*Also: merc tribunal*

**A secret military court that the remaining mercenaries in Martinaise are organising to punish whoever they blame for the lynching.**

Joyce Messier warns you about it: the surviving contractors are running their own investigation into the hanging, and once they are done, executions will follow. Kim sees it as the reason to solve the case fast, before there is blood on the streets.

See also: [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [Wild Pines](#wild-pines), [Joyce Messier](#joyce-messier), [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [Lynching](#lynching)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

The tribunal eventually arrives at the Whirling-in-Rags, and the confrontation turns into a deadly shootout in which you are shot in the leg.

</details>

### ONE DAY I WILL RETURN TO YOUR SIDE

**A giant, freshly painted graffito that appears on the square outside the Whirling-in-Rags; it smells of fuel oil and blood.**

The letters are painted in red-dyed heavy fuel oil, mopped onto the ground in huge strokes. Kim calls it an 'aerograffito', meant to be read from the aerostatics passing over the city, and connects it to the whispers about 'The Return' heard all over Revachol West.

Interfacing points out that fuel oil is flammable.

See also: [Whirling-in-Rags](#whirling-in-rags), [Klaasje](#klaasje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It is painted over the bloody traces of the fight outside the Whirling, probably by Cindy the SKULL. Lighting it with a cigarette sets it ablaze for a moment.

</details>

### Oranjese Passport

**A passport from the Republic of Oranje that Klaasje hid in a plastic buoy out in the reeds along the coast.**

Klaasje tells you where she hid it, and you can fish it out of the buoy. The photo shows her with short black hair and glasses.

See also: [Klaasje](#klaasje), [Oranje](#oranje)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It is issued to 'Katarzine Alasije', not to Annouk Meijer-Smit as she claimed. Kim concludes that neither name, nor Klaasje, is likely to be her real identity.

</details>

### Pale Latitude Compressor

*Also: Latitude Compressor, Pale Compressor*

**Esoteric radio technology that forces a radio-signal grid onto the pale to 'crunch' distances across it; being caught in its field is agony.**

Near a working compressor your head fills with a storm of static, numbers and stray voices, and your brain feels like it is swelling. The pain eases once the device is switched off or broken.

See also: [Pale](#pale), [Radiocomputer](#radiocomputer)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Ruby uses one in her hideout to overpower you and Kim when you find her.

</details>

### Ruby

*Also: Ruby, the Instigator*

**A woman connected to the Hardie boys who knows a lot about what happened.**

See also: [Hardie boys](#hardie-boys), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

Ruby helped stage the hanging after the man was already dead. She is not the killer.

</details>

### Ruud Hoenkloewen

*Also: Ruud*

**A silent mercenary in ceramic armour and a full helmet, armed with a long rifle; his comrades call him 'The Killer'.**

He barely speaks, and when he does it comes out as a muffled shout from inside his helmet. Little stick figures counted along the rim of the helmet hint at his record.

See also: [Mercenaries](#mercenaries), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man), [Scab Leader](#scab-leader)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He is one of the three armed comrades of the dead man who come to Martinaise for revenge and confront you at the tribunal, alongside Kortenaer and De Paule.

</details>

### Triangong

*Also: Triangong 4-46, Triangong 4.46*

**A rifle model, the Triangong 4.46, made in Hsin-Yao. You only come across one very late in the case.**

The make and origin are burnt into its wooden butt, along with the slogan 'For Safre, and for all mankind'.

See also: [Antique rifle](#antique-rifle), [The Hanged Man](#the-hanged-man)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

It belongs to Iosef Lilianovich Dros, the Deserter, who used it to shoot the Hanged Man from about a kilometre away across the water. You can pick it up from the sand and keep it.

</details>

### Victor Méjean

*Also: Victor*

**The man found dead on the Martinaise boardwalk; he fell through a rotten plank and struck his head on a metal bench, an empty vodka bottle beside him.**

His body lies by a hole in the creaking boardwalk with a chewing gum wrapper clutched in his fist, and a library card issued to someone named Billie Méjean in his pocket.

See also: [Billie Méjean](#billie-méjean), [Capeside Apartments](#capeside-apartments)

<details><summary>Spoiler</summary>

He is Billie Méjean's husband. He was often in some kind of trouble, and he had borrowed books on his wife's library card, which is how the card leads you to their apartment in the Capeside Apartments.

</details>

</details>
